//! Eligibility suppression binds every checked column to the same created entity.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#169-day-two-precision-and-destructive-evidence-questions — checking the inspector is not checking the applicant.
use super::*;
pub(super) type Indexes=BTreeMap<(String,String),BTreeSet<String>>;
pub(super) fn indexes(bound:&Bound)->Indexes{
    let mut out=Indexes::new();for p in &bound.programs{for r in snapback4_core::ir::read_sites(&p.program){let site=p.sources.get(&r.site).map(|(site,_)|site.to_string()).unwrap_or(r.site);out.entry((r.table,site)).or_default().insert(r.index);}}out
}
fn primary(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,table:&str,site:&Site,keys:&[ast::KeyPart])->bool{
    if keys.len()!=1||keys[0].column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){return false;}
    if keys[0].column.as_deref()==Some("id"){return true;}
    indexes.get(&(table.into(),site.to_string())).is_some_and(|names|names.len()==1&&names.iter().all(|name|bound.schema.tables[table].index(name).is_some_and(|i|i.columns()==["id"])))
}
fn read(e:&Expr)->Option<&ast::ReadExpr>{match e{Expr::Read(r)=>Some(r),Expr::Assert{value,..}=>read(value),_=>None}}
fn id_value(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,e:&Expr,p:&ast::ProgramDecl,table:&str)->Option<String>{
    match e{
        Expr::Name{name,..} if p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*name&&matches!(&a.type_.kind,TypeKind::Ref{table:t} if t==table))=>Some(name.clone()),
        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="id"=>{let r=read(base)?;if r.table!=table||!primary(bound,indexes,table,&r.site,&r.keys){return None;}
            if r.keys[0].column.as_deref()==Some("id"){named_id(bound,indexes,&r.keys[0].value,p,table)}else{id_value(bound,indexes,&r.keys[0].value,p,table)}},
        Expr::Assert{value,..}=>id_value(bound,indexes,value,p,table),_=>None,
    }
}
fn row_id(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,r:&ast::ReadExpr,p:&ast::ProgramDecl)->Option<String>{
    if !primary(bound,indexes,&r.table,&r.site,&r.keys){return None;}if r.keys[0].column.as_deref()==Some("id"){named_id(bound,indexes,&r.keys[0].value,p,&r.table)}else{id_value(bound,indexes,&r.keys[0].value,p,&r.table)}
}
fn named_id(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,e:&Expr,p:&ast::ProgramDecl,table:&str)->Option<String>{
    match e{Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(_),..}=>Some(crate::print::expression(e)),_=>id_value(bound,indexes,e,p,table)}
}
fn stable_field(bound:&Bound,creator:&Write<'_>,value:&Expr)->Option<String>{
    if !matches!(value,Expr::Literal{..})&&!matches!(value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"||creator.p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*name)){return None;}
    creator.fields.iter().filter(|f|bound.schema.tables[&creator.table].immutable_fields().any(|c|c==f.name)).find(|f|field_value(f).is_some_and(|e|crate::print::expression(e)==crate::print::expression(value))).map(|f|f.name.clone())
}
fn normalize(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,creator:&Write<'_>,current:&Write<'_>,e:&Expr,creation:bool)->Option<String>{
    let mut e=e.clone();let origins=if current.table==creator.table&&current.op==WriteOp::Update{
        current.keys.first().filter(|_|primary(bound,indexes,&current.table,&current.site,&current.keys)).and_then(|k|if k.column.as_deref()==Some("id"){named_id(bound,indexes,&k.value,current.p,&creator.table)}else{id_value(bound,indexes,&k.value,current.p,&creator.table)}).into_iter().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()
    }else{
        current.fields.iter().filter(|f|matches!(bound.schema.tables[&current.table].columns.get(&f.name).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Ref{table}) if table==&creator.table)).filter_map(field_value).filter_map(|e|id_value(bound,indexes,e,current.p,&creator.table)).collect()
    };
    super::super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut e,&mut |e|{
        let field=if creation{stable_field(bound,creator,e)}else if let Expr::Member{base,name,..}=e{
            read(base).filter(|_|bound.schema.tables[&creator.table].immutable_fields().any(|c|c==name)).filter(|r|r.table==creator.table&&row_id(bound,indexes,r,current.p).is_some_and(|id|origins.contains(&id))).and_then(|_|creator.fields.iter().find(|f|f.name==*name)).and_then(field_value).and_then(|v|stable_field(bound,creator,v))
        }else{None};
        if let Some(field)=field{*e=Expr::Name{name:format!("$created_{field}"),site:e.site().clone()};}
    });
    fn known(e:&Expr)->bool{match e{Expr::Literal{..}=>true,Expr::Name{name,..}=>name.starts_with("$created_"),Expr::Assert{value,..}=>known(value),_=>false}}
    known(&e).then(||crate::print::expression(&e))
}
fn identity(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,creator:&Write<'_>,current:&Write<'_>,r:&ast::ReadExpr,creation:bool)->Option<String>{
    let t=bound.schema.tables.get(&r.table)?;
    let names=indexes.get(&(r.table.clone(),r.site.to_string()))?;if names.len()!=1{return None;}
    let index=t.index(names.first()?)?;if !index.unique||!index.is_plain(){return None;}let columns=index.columns();
    let mut pins=BTreeMap::new();
    for (position,k) in r.keys.iter().enumerate(){let column=k.column.clone().or_else(||columns.get(position).cloned())?;pins.insert(column,normalize(bound,indexes,creator,current,&k.value,creation)?);}
    Some(serde_json::to_string(&columns.iter().map(|c|Some((c,pins.get(c)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?).unwrap())
}
pub(super) fn checks(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,creator:&Write<'_>,current:&Write<'_>,writes:&[Write<'_>],creation:bool)->BTreeMap<String,BTreeSet<(String,String)>>{
    let mut out=BTreeMap::<String,BTreeSet<(String,String)>>::new();
    if !creation&&writes.iter().any(|w|std::ptr::eq(w.p,current.p)&&w.table==creator.table&&matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert|WriteOp::Delete)){return out;}

    let mutable=|table:&str,column:&str|bound.schema.tables.get(table).is_some_and(|t|t.columns.contains_key(column)&&!t.immutable_fields().any(|c|c==column))&&writes.iter().any(|o|o.table==table&&(o.op==WriteOp::Delete||matches!(o.op,WriteOp::Update|WriteOp::Upsert)&&o.fields.iter().any(|f|f.name==column)));
    for condition in conditions(&current.context){walk(condition,|e|{
        let (r,columns)=match e{
            Expr::Member{base,name,..}=>{let Some(r)=read(base)else{return;};(r,vec![name.clone()])},
            Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=>{let mut cols=r.keys.iter().filter_map(|k|k.column.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();if writes.iter().any(|o|o.table==r.table&&o.op==WriteOp::Delete){cols.push("existence".into());}(r,cols)},_=>return,
        };
        if r.table==creator.table||!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&r.table).is_empty(){return;}
        let entity=match identity(bound,indexes,creator,current,r,creation){Some(entity)=>entity,None if creation=>format!("$unknown:{}",crate::print::expression(&Expr::Read(r.clone()))),None=>return};
        for column in columns{if column=="existence"||mutable(&r.table,&column){out.entry(r.table.clone()).or_default().insert((column,entity.clone()));}}
    });}out
}
//! Individually justified source-bound dispositions; never proof or authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#162-narrow-gates-and-individual-dispositions — bulk compiler-note review cannot clear security gates.
use super::*;
use serde::{Serialize,Deserialize};
use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};
const ANALYZER:&str="sec7-6";
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct File{version:u32,entries:BTreeMap<String,Entry>}
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Entry{sources:String,analyzer:String,reason:String}
#[derive(Serialize)]
pub(super) struct Disposition{pub id:String,pub code:String,pub site:String,pub gated:bool,pub acknowledged:bool,pub reason:Option<String>}
pub(super) fn gated(code:&str)->bool{matches!(code,"N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT"|"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE")}
fn hash(bytes:&[u8])->String{format!("{:x}",Sha256::digest(bytes))}
fn digest(s:&str)->bool{s.len()==64&&s.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_hexdigit())}
fn id(n:&snapback4_lang::Diagnostic)->String{hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&serde_json::to_value(n).unwrap()).unwrap())}
pub(super) fn review(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>)->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    if project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed during security review; rerun check".into());}
    let mut sorted=sources.to_vec();sorted.sort_by(|a,b|a.0.cmp(&b.0));
    let fingerprint=hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&sorted).unwrap());
    let path=project.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json");
    // Keep the lock inode in ignored generated output. Do not unlink a lock
    // while another process can be waiting on that inode. Kernel ownership
    // releases it on exit, including an interrupted writer.
    let _writer_lock=if ack.is_some(){
        let directory=project.schema_dir().join("generated");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&directory).map_err(|e|format!("cannot create security review lock directory: {e}"))?;
        let lock=std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).truncate(false).read(true).write(true).open(directory.join("security-reviews.lock")).map_err(|e|format!("cannot open security review lock: {e}"))?;
        lock.lock().map_err(|e|format!("cannot acquire security review lock: {e}"))?;Some(lock)
    }else{None};
    if project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed while waiting for security review; rerun check".into());}
    let mut file:File=match std::fs::read(&path){
        Ok(bytes)=>serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_|"invalid snapback/security-reviews.json; expected version 1 with per-note source/analyzer/reason records".to_string())?,
        Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound=>File{version:1,entries:BTreeMap::new()},
        Err(e)=>return Err(format!("cannot read security dispositions: {e}")),
    };
    if file.version!=1||file.entries.iter().any(|(key,e)|!digest(key)||!digest(&e.sources)||e.reason.trim().is_empty()||e.analyzer.is_empty()){return Err("invalid security disposition version/digest/reason".into());}
    if let Some((key,reason))=ack{
        if reason.trim().is_empty(){return Err("--reason needs the specification rationale for this individual note".into());}
        if !notes.iter().any(|n|id(n)==key){return Err("--ack-security ID is not a current security note; run check --json".into());}
        file.entries.insert(key.into(),Entry{sources:fingerprint.clone(),analyzer:ANALYZER.into(),reason:reason.trim().into()});
        let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("json.{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
        let result=std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&file).unwrap()).and_then(|_|{
            if project.sources()?!=sources{return Err(std::io::Error::other("Quarry sources changed before disposition publication; rerun check"));}
            std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path)
        });
        if let Err(e)=result{let _=std::fs::remove_file(&temporary);return Err(format!("cannot record security disposition: {e}"));}
    }
    Ok(notes.iter().map(|n|{let key=id(n);let entry=file.entries.get(&key).filter(|e|e.sources==fingerprint&&e.analyzer==ANALYZER);
        Disposition{id:key,code:n.code.clone(),site:n.site.to_string(),gated:gated(&n.code),acknowledged:entry.is_some(),reason:entry.map(|e|e.reason.clone())}
    }).collect())
}
pub(super) fn pending(dispositions:&[Disposition])->bool{dispositions.iter().any(|d|d.gated&&!d.acknowledged)}
pub(super) fn print(dispositions:&[Disposition]){for d in dispositions.iter().filter(|d|d.gated||d.acknowledged){
    if d.acknowledged{println!("security reviewed {} {}: {}",d.id,d.code,d.reason.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));}
    else{println!("security review required {} {} at {}; repair or check --ack-security {} --reason '<spec rationale>'",d.id,d.code,d.site,d.id);}
}}

    45	}

     1	//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
     3	use super::*;
     4	const LIMIT:usize=512;
     5	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
     6	pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}
     7	
     8	pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
     9	    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    10	    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    11	    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
    12	        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
    13	        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
    14	        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    15	    }}
    16	    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    17	    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    18	    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    19	    out.into_iter().collect()
    20	}
    21	pub(super) fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    22	    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    23	    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
    24	        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    25	    }}}
    26	    out
    27	}
    28	pub(super) fn fixture_cases(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    29	    let args=&p.args;
    30	    let mut literals=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<Value>>::new();
    31	    fn collect(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
    32	        if let Some(binary)=value.get("Binary") {for (a,b) in [("left","right"),("right","left")] {
    33	            if let (Some(name),Some(literal))=(binary.get(a).and_then(|v|v.get("Variable")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binary.get(b).and_then(|v|v.get("Literal"))) {
    34	                if let Ok(value)=serde_json::from_value::<Value>(literal.clone()){out.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(value);}
    35	            }
    36	        }}
    37	        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){collect(v,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{collect(v,out)},_=>{}}
    38	    }
    39	    collect(&serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap(),&mut literals);
    40	    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
    41	        Some(match k{
    42	            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
    43	            ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..}=>['z','y'].into_iter().map(|c|Value::String(c.to_string().repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize))).collect(),
    44	            ir::ArgType::Int=>vec![Value::Int(0),Value::Int(1),Value::Int(2)],
    45	            ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max}=>{let mut xs=vec![*min,1i64.clamp(*min,*max),*max];xs.sort();xs.dedup();xs.into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()},
    46	            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
    47	            ir::ArgType::Time=>vec![Value::Int(NOW-86_400_000),Value::Int(NOW),Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000)],
    48	            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
    49	            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
    50	            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
    51	            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
    52	        })
    53	    }
    54	    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    55	    for (name,k) in args{
    56	        let Some(mut vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
    57	        for literal in literals.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
    58	            let valid=match (k,literal){
    59	                (ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..},Value::String(s))=>s.chars().filter(|c|!c.is_whitespace()).count()>=*min as usize&&s.chars().count()<=*max as usize,
    60	                (ir::ArgType::Int,Value::Int(_))=>true,
    61	                (ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>(*min..=*max).contains(n),_=>false,
    62	            };
    63	            if valid&&!vals.contains(literal){vals.insert(0,literal.clone());}
    64	        }
    65	        let mut next=Vec::new();
    66	        'product:for offset in 0..vals.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(vals.len()){
    67	            if out.exhausted(){return next;}
    68	            let mut case=cases[n%cases.len()].clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[(n+offset)%vals.len()].clone());
    69	            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
    70	        }}
    71	        cases=next;
    72	    }
    73	    let focused=super::executed::write_arguments(bound,p).into_iter().filter(|a|matches!(p.args.get(a),Some(ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)) if !matches!(inner.as_ref(),ir::ArgType::Ref{..}|ir::ArgType::Principal))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    74	    if focused.is_empty(){return cases;}
    75	    let mut prioritized=Vec::new();
    76	    for case in cases{
    77	        for name in &focused{if let Some(value)=arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.clone(),p.args[name].clone())]),4).and_then(|mut a|a.remove(name))&&value!=Value::Null{
    78	            let mut alternate=case.clone();alternate.insert(name.clone(),value);if !prioritized.contains(&alternate){prioritized.push(alternate);}
    79	        }}
    80	        if !prioritized.contains(&case){prioritized.push(case.clone());}
    81	        if prioritized.len()>=LIMIT{prioritized.truncate(LIMIT);out.unsupported.insert("column-write argument frontier capped at 512; remaining combinations uncovered".into());break;}
    82	    }prioritized
    83	}
    84	// Preserve status/decision alternatives on each resource tuple before the
    85	// small directed cap; text/noise arguments must not correlate bools with ids.
    86	fn directed_cases(p:&Program,cases:Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>,campaign:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    87	    let identities=p.args.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Ref{..}|ir::ArgType::Principal)).map(|(n,_)|n).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    88	    let original=cases.clone();
    89	    let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
    90	    for base in cases{let key=serde_json::to_string(&identities.iter().map(|n|(*n,base.get(*n))).collect::<Vec<_>>()).unwrap();if !seen.insert(key){continue;}
    91	        let mut group=vec![base.clone()];
    92	        for (name,kind) in &p.args{let values=match kind{ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().cloned().map(Value::String).collect(),_=>Vec::new()};
    93	            for value in values{let mut candidate=base.clone();candidate.insert(name.clone(),value);if !group.contains(&candidate){group.push(candidate);}}
    94	        }groups.push(group);
    95	    }
    96	    let mut out=Vec::new();for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in &groups{if let Some(args)=group.get(n){out.push(args.clone());}}}
    97	    if original.iter().any(|case|!out.contains(case)){campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: directed argument grouping omits correlated/non-status alternatives; remaining combinations uncovered",p.name));}
    98	    out
    99	}
   100	pub(super) fn trace_key(steps:&[Step])->Vec<(&str,&str)>{steps.iter().map(|s|(s.operation.as_str(),s.viewer.as_str())).collect()}
   101	pub(super) fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str)->bool{
   102	    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){return false;}
   103	    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});true
   104	}
   105	pub(super) fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
   106	    typed_disclosure_image(ty,data,table,key,column,physical,None)
   107	}
   108	pub(super) fn typed_disclosure_image(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value,policy:Option<&RuleExpr>)->bool{
   109	    use crate::types::Ty;
   110	    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)))&&policy.is_none_or(|p|!p.any_term(&|term|{
   111	        let column=match term{RuleTerm::Field(c)|RuleTerm::OldField(c)|RuleTerm::NextField(c)=>Some(c.as_str()),RuleTerm::Reference{column,..}|RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{column,..}=>Some(column.as_str()),_=>None};
   112	        column.filter(|c|*c!="$security:server-clock").is_some_and(|c|data.field(c).is_err()||data.field(c).ok()!=physical.field(c).ok())
   113	    }));
   114	    match ty{
   115	        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
   116	        Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::Omittable(inner)=>typed_disclosure_image(inner,data,table,key,column,physical,policy),
   117	        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
   118	        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure_image(inner,r,table,key,column,physical,policy))),
   119	        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure_image(ty,v,table,key,column,physical,policy))),
   120	        _=>false,
   121	    }
   122	}
   123	pub(super) fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
   124	    use crate::types::Ty;
   125	    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::Omittable(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
   126	}
   127	pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
   128	    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
   129	    loop{let before=out.len();
   130	        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
   131	        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||super::super::columns::rules(t).any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
   132	        if out.len()==before{return out;}
   133	    }
   134	}
   135	fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
   136	    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
   137	    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
   138	}
   139	fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
   140	    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
   141	    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
   142	        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
   143	        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
   144	            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
   145	        }else{true}
   146	    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   147	    if queries.is_empty(){return;}

   218	fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
   219	    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
   220	}
   221	fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   222	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
   223	        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
   224	            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
   225	            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
   226	            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
   227	            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
   228	            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
   229	                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
   230	            }
   231	        }
   232	    }
   233	}
   234	fn changed(bound:&Bound,_p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   235	    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
   236	    // Disappearance is coverage evidence only. Actual delete authority is
   237	    // judged by the executed-write trace, never by a commit-end image diff.
   238	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|matches!(i.target.as_str(),"delete"|"write")){
   239	        if bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].is_expiring()&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|new|new.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
   240	            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: implicit retirement is not an executed delete; delete intent requires an authored write boundary",inv.target_label()));
   241	        }
   242	    }
   243	}

   245	fn role_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
   246	    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
   247	    let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
   248	    if !ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(table,_)|!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table).is_empty()){return out;}
   249	    if !campaign.reserve(1){return Vec::new();}
   250	    let Ok(mut judge)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return Vec::new();};
   251	    for (table,_) in ir::write_tables(p){
   252	        let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&table);
   253	        if !columns.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))){continue;}
   254	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
   255	        for row in initial.get(&table).into_iter().flatten(){
   256	            let principal=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal).then(||row.field(c).ok()).flatten());
   257	            let Some(principal)=principal else{continue;};
   258	            let mut targets=Vec::new();
   259	            for case in cases{
   260	                let mut args=case.clone();
   261	                for (name,kind) in &p.args{match kind{
   262	                    ir::ArgType::Principal=>{args.insert(name.clone(),principal.clone());},
   263	                    ir::ArgType::Ref{table:target} if target==&table=>{if let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(name.clone(),id.clone());}},_=>{}
   264	                }}
   265	                if columns.iter().any(|c|args.get(c).is_some_and(|v|row.field(c).ok()==Some(v))){continue;}
   266	                if !targets.contains(&args){targets.push(args);}
   267	            }
   268	            let mut row_cases=Vec::new();
   269	            for actor in actors{
   270	                let owner=columns.iter().filter_map(|c|t.written_by.get(c)).any(|a|row.field(&a.owner).ok()==Some(&Value::Id(actor.clone())));
   271	                if !campaign.reserve(1){return out;}
   272	                let gate=judge.fixture_policy(&t.update_rule_judged(),actor,Some(row),row,NOW).ok();
   273	                if !owner&&gate!=Some(rules::Verdict::Readable){continue;}
   274	                for args in &targets{row_cases.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
   275	            }
   276	            groups.push(row_cases);
   277	        }
   278	    }
   279	    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(case)=g.get(n){out.push(case.clone());}}}
   280	    out
   281	}
   282	// Prioritize actually admitted relationship changes rather than filling the
   283	// history cap with cross-products rejected by a recipient-role require.
   284	fn relationship_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
   285	    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
   286	    if !p.args.values().any(|k|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Principal))||!writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()&&bound.schema.tables[t].columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal))){return Vec::new();}
   287	    let mut candidates=Vec::new();let mut attempts=0;
   288	    let Ok(mut judge)=fixture.fork_fixture()else{return candidates;};
   289	    for actor in actors{
   290	        let allowed=writes.iter().any(|(t,kind)|super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()&&initial.get(t).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|{
   291	            let table=&bound.schema.tables[t];let gates=if *kind=="insert"{vec![&table.rules.insert]}else{vec![&table.rules.insert,&table.rules.update]};
   292	            gates.into_iter().any(|gate|matches!(judge.fixture_policy(gate,actor,Some(row),row,NOW),Ok(rules::Verdict::Readable)))
   293	        }));
   294	        if !allowed{continue;}
   295	        for args in cases{
   296	            if attempts>=128||candidates.len()>=16||!out.reserve(1){return candidates;}attempts+=1;
   297	            let Ok(mut e)=fixture.fork_fixture()else{continue;};
   298	            let Ok(materialized)=materialize_arguments(&mut e,&p.args,args,actor)else{continue;};
   299	            if !matches!(e.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(actor,materialized).at(NOW)),Ok(r) if matches!(r.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})){continue;}
   300	            let Ok(after)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
   301	            if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|initial.get(t).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(t).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|bound.schema.tables[t].columns.iter().any(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))){
   302	                candidates.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));
   303	            }
   304	        }
   305	    }candidates
   306	}
   307	pub(super) fn initial_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   308	    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
   309	    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
   310	    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   311	    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
   312	    let Ok(mut engine)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return;};
   313	    observe(bound,&mut engine,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
   314	}
   315	// Prefer real lifecycle successors before the general diagonal history matrix.
   316	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#163-directed-finite-histories — pruning orders finite replay, never proves safety.
   317	pub(super) fn lifecycle_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   318	    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted()||bound.invariants.iter().all(|i|out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==i.site.to_string()&&v.reachability.is_reachable())){return;}

   321	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&i.table];let gate=match i.target.as_str(){"read"=>super::super::columns::admission(t,i.column.as_deref()),"insert"=>t.rules.insert.clone(),"update"=>t.update_rule_judged().into_owned(),_=>RuleExpr::Allow};
   322	        i.policy.as_ref().is_none_or(|policy|kernel::implies(&gate,policy)!=Ok(true))
   323	    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   324	    if interesting.is_empty(){return;}
   325	    let selected_sites=interesting.iter().map(|i|i.site.to_string()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   326	    let mut relevant=interesting.into_iter().flat_map(|i|std::iter::once(i.table.clone()).chain(i.policy.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies))).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();

   328	    let has_lifecycle=relevant.iter().any(|name|bound.schema.tables[name].columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Enum{..}|C::Bool|C::Principal|C::Ref{..})));
   329	    if !has_lifecycle{return;}
   330	    let transitions=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!bp.program.native&&!bound.schema.operations.get(&bp.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral)).filter(|bp|{
   331	        ir::write_tables(&bp.program).iter().any(|(name,kind)|relevant.contains(name)&&(*kind!="insert"||bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|&i.table==name)))

   342	    fn signature(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->String{
   343	        // Equality of this projection is only an exploration heuristic. Missing
   344	        // worlds are explicitly uncovered; it never discharges an obligation.

   346	    }

   350	        for (base,steps) in frontier{
   351	            let Ok(before)=readback(&base)else{continue;};

   353	                let p=&bp.program;
   354	                let targets=ir::write_tables(p);

   390	                    if !matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..}){out.refused+=1;continue;}
   391	                    let Ok(after)=readback(&engine)else{continue;};
   392	                    let mut history=steps.clone();history.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
   393	                    super::executed::record(bound,p,&trace,&result.state,&after,&initial,viewer,&history,&mut engine,out);
   394	                    changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&history,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);
   395	                    if selected_sites.iter().all(|site|out.violations.iter().any(|v|&v.invariant_site==site&&v.reachability.is_reachable())){
   396	                        out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle stopped after every selected independent intent had a replayed violation; additional histories uncovered".into());return;
   397	                    }
   398	                    if before==after{continue;}
   399	                    out.sequences+=1;
   400	                    if !seen.insert(signature(bound,&after)){out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle signature merges worlds differing in non-status payload; remaining worlds uncovered".into());continue;}
   401	                    observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&history,out);
   402	                    column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&history,out);
   403	                    if selected_sites.iter().all(|site|out.violations.iter().any(|v|&v.invariant_site==site&&v.reachability.is_reachable())){
   404	                        out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle stopped after every selected independent intent had a replayed violation; additional histories uncovered".into());return;
   405	                    }
   406	                    let progress=after.iter().filter(|(name,_)|relevant.contains(*name)).map(|(name,rs)|{let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];rs.iter().map(|r|t.columns.iter().map(|(c,k)|match k.required(){C::Enum{..} if !super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name).contains(c)=>before.get(name).into_iter().flatten().find(|old|old.field("id").ok()==r.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|old|old.field(c).ok()!=r.field(c).ok()) as usize*4,C::Bool=>matches!(r.field(c),Ok(Value::Bool(true))) as usize,_=>0}).sum::<usize>()).sum::<usize>()}).sum::<usize>();
   407	                    let state_key=signature(bound,&after.iter().filter(|(name,_)|relevant.contains(*name)).map(|(name,rs)|(name.clone(),rs.clone())).collect());
   408	                    if successors.iter().any(|(_,_,_,key)|key==&state_key){out.unsupported.insert("directed successor diversity merges audit-only attribution variants; additional histories uncovered".into());continue;}
   409	                    successors.push((engine,history,progress,state_key));
   410	                    out.directed_peak_worlds=out.directed_peak_worlds.max(frontier_count+next.len()+successors.len()+1);
   411	                    successors.sort_by_key(|(_,_,priority,_)|std::cmp::Reverse(*priority));
   412	                    if successors.len()>2{successors.truncate(2);out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: directed successor selection keeps two priority resource states per operation/world; other states uncovered",p.name));}
   413	                }}
   414	                for (engine,history,priority,_) in successors{
   415	                    next.push((engine,history,priority));
   416	                    out.directed_peak_worlds=out.directed_peak_worlds.max(frontier_count+next.len()+1);
   417	                    next.sort_by_key(|(_,_,priority)|std::cmp::Reverse(*priority));
   418	                    if next.len()>4{next.truncate(4);out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle beam keeps four priority worlds; displaced worlds dropped immediately and remain uncovered".into());}
   419	                }
   420	            }
   421	        }
   422	        if next.is_empty(){return;}
   423	        frontier=next.into_iter().map(|(e,h,_)|(e,h)).collect();
   424	    }
   425	    out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle traces bounded to eight authored mutations; longer traces uncovered".into());
   426	}
   427	pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   428	    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
   429	    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   430	    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
   431	    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   432	    let probes=Vec::new();
   433	    let mut groups=Vec::new();
   434	    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native&&!bound.schema.operations.get(&p.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral)){
   435	        let cases=fixture_cases(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,out);
   436	        let mut group=relationship_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture,out);
   437	        group.extend(role_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture,out));
   438	        // Diagonal order gives both actors and argument values an early turn.
   439	        let mut seen=group.iter().map(|(_,viewer,args)|serde_json::to_string(&(viewer,args)).unwrap()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   440	        // Give every actor the same first argument tuple before diagonal
   441	        // variation; a referee must not see only the overturned bout by chance.
   442	        if let Some(args)=cases.first(){for actor in &actors{
   443	            if group.len()>=LIMIT||out.exhausted(){break;}
   444	            if seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(actor,args)).unwrap()){group.push((&bp.program,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
   445	        }}
   446	        'diagonal:for shift in 0..actors.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(actors.len()){
   447	            if cases.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){break 'diagonal;}
   448	            if group.len()>=LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history candidate actors/arguments capped at 512",bp.program.name));break 'diagonal;}
   449	            let actor=&actors[(n+shift)%actors.len()];let args=&cases[n%cases.len()];
   450	            if seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(actor,args)).unwrap()){group.push((&bp.program,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
   451	        }}
   452	        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
   453	    }
   454	    let mut candidates=Vec::new();let mut candidate_keys=BTreeSet::new();
   455	    // Front-load a small fair frontier of role and relationship changes so
   456	    // ordinary no-op/rejected mutations cannot consume all observer work first.
   457	    for group in &groups {
   458	        let p=group[0].0;
   459	        let priority=ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,kind)|*kind!="insert"&&(!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()||p.args.values().any(|k|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Principal))));
   460	        if priority{for c in group.iter().take(8){if candidate_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&c.0.name,&c.1,&c.2)).unwrap()){candidates.push(c.clone());if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}}}}
   461	        if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}
   462	    }
   463	    'candidates:for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){if candidate_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&c.0.name,&c.1,&c.2)).unwrap()){candidates.push(c.clone());}if candidates.len()==LIMIT{break 'candidates;}}}}
   464	    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
   465	    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   466	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
   467	        for row in initial.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){for viewer in &actors{
   468	            if !out.reserve(1){return;}
   469	            if policy_in(bound,inv,&initial,viewer,None,row,&mut base)!=Ok(false){continue;}
   470	            let mut witness=Campaign::default();violation(&mut witness,inv,&[],viewer,row,None,&initial,"always");
   471	            if let Some(mut v)=witness.violations.pop(){v.reachability=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace;v.operation="$fixture.assertion".into();v.rule_path="initial trusted fixture assertion; no authored mutation".into();v.scope="definite violation in authored fixture; initialization trusted, no authored transition claimed".into();out.violations.push(v);}break;
   472	        }}
   473	    }
   474	    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
   475	    // Spend half the pair budget on grant changes followed by an operation
   476	    // as the affected principal; interleave first steps and successor names.
   477	    let mut role_groups=Vec::new();
   478	    for (i,(p,_,args)) in candidates.iter().enumerate().take(LIMIT){
   479	        let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
   480	        let rosters=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter_map(|(t,_)|{
   481	            let roles=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&t);
   482	            roles.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))).then_some(t)
   483	        }).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   484	        if rosters.is_empty(){continue;}
   485	        let mut recipients=BTreeSet::new();
   486	        for (name,kind) in &p.args {if let Some(value)=args.get(name){match kind {
   487	            ir::ArgType::Principal=>{if let Value::Id(actor)=value{recipients.insert(actor.clone());}},
   488	            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rosters.contains(table)=>{
   489	                if let Some(row)=initial.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(value)){
   490	                    for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(kind.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(actor))=row.field(column){recipients.insert(actor.clone());}}
   491	                }
   492	            },_=>{}
   493	        }}}
   494	        let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
   495	        let successors=candidates.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(j,(next,actor,_))|(next.name!=p.name&&recipients.contains(actor)&&seen.insert((next.name.clone(),actor.clone()))).then_some((i,j))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   496	        if !successors.is_empty(){role_groups.push(successors);}
   497	    }
   498	    let mut role_pairs=Vec::new();
   499	    'pairs:for n in 0..role_groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in &role_groups{if let Some(pair)=group.get(n){role_pairs.push(*pair);if role_pairs.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'pairs;}}}}
   500	    // Breadth first, then targeted and diagonal pairs: no first step owns the cap.
   501	    let history_limit=if out.history_limit==0{LIMIT}else{out.history_limit.min(LIMIT)};
   502	    let singles=candidates.len().min(history_limit);
   503	    let mut setups=Vec::new();let mut role_changes=Vec::new();let mut committed=Vec::new();
   504	    let mut setup_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut role_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut pair_plan=Vec::new();
   505	    for n in 0..singles+history_limit {
   506	        if !out.reserve(1){break;}
   507	        if n==singles&&!candidates.is_empty(){
   508	            let disclosures=super::executed::pairs(bound,&candidates,&committed);
   509	            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
   510	            for pair in disclosures.into_iter().take(LIMIT/2){if seen.insert(pair){pair_plan.push(pair);}}
   511	            'setup:for &second in &role_changes{for &first in &setups{if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'setup;}}}}
   512	            for &(first,second) in &role_pairs{if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT{break;}if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));}}
   513	            let mut k=0;
   514	            while pair_plan.len()<LIMIT{pair_plan.push((k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()));k+=1;}
   515	        }
   516	        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let (first,second)=pair_plan[n-singles];vec![first,second]};
   517	        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   518	        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
   519	        for i in indices{
   520	            if !out.reserve(1){break;}
   521	            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   522	            let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history asset arguments: {why}",p.name));break;}};
   523	            let mut prior=if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){if !out.reserve(1){break;}match fork(bound,&engine,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>Some(e),Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);None}}}else{None};
   524	            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   525	            let trace=super::executed::trace(bound,p);
   526	            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW).with_fixture_trace(trace.clone())){
   527	                Ok(result)=>{
   528	                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
   529	                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
   530	                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   531	                    if sent&&n<singles{
   532	                        if before!=after{
   533	                            let transition=bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(name,t)|before.get(name).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(name).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|t.columns.iter().any(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Enum{..})&&old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok()))));
   534	                            if transition{committed.insert(0,i);}else{committed.push(i);}
   535	                        }
   536	                        let signature=serde_json::to_string(&(&p.name,viewer,&candidates[i].2)).unwrap();
   537	                        let new_media=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|inv|inv.target=="read"&&initial.get(&inv.table).is_none_or(Vec::is_empty)).any(|inv|{
   538	                            let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
   539	                            after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|table.columns.iter().any(|(column,kind)|kind.contains_asset()&&row.field(column).is_ok_and(|v|*v!=Value::Null&&!matches!(v,Value::Array(xs) if xs.is_empty()))))
   540	                        });
   541	                        if new_media&&setup_seen.insert(signature.clone()){setups.push(i);}
   542	                        let changed_role=bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(table,_)|{
   543	                            let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
   544	                            !columns.is_empty()&&before.get(table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|columns.iter().any(|c|old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))
   545	                        });
   546	                        if changed_role&&role_seen.insert(signature){role_changes.push(i);}
   547	                    }
   548	                    if sent{super::executed::record(bound,p,&trace,&result.state,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut engine,out);changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
   549	                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
   550	                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
   551	            }
   552	        }
   553	    }
   554	    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
   555	}

   563	}}

   123	 assert!(has(s,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));

Commit snapback/security-reviews.json when the decision should travel with source.

again; the record never grants authority or proves safety. Audit retains the full

Optional arguments and optional columns are valid, including deliberate loosening.
A required child media column validates an existing attachment; it does not require
a child row. Guarded optional attachments need no acknowledgment. When independent
parent integrity intent requires a child, require evidence before creating that
parent. Without such intent, a conditional child is an advisory spec question. Reasons use text 1..N when nonempty justification is required; optional
money and constant payment/approval flags need independent specification review.
Transition advice asks whether a generic setter can bypass a stricter decision or

Role-wide write questions ask whether a global role may write every resource.

fixtures retain their generated-fixture-trace origin and can fail acceptance.

### N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT

N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT: A recipient role required when storing a grant is mutable and missing from a reader branch that trusts that grant, including column payload readers. Recheck current role or revoke derived grants. Intentional retained authority needs an individual source-bound disposition.

### N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE

N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE: An optional upload argument can be omitted in a direct nullable-media write or a skipped child that independent intent requires. Require the upload before creating the parent when independent intent requires child evidence. Optional media columns and guarded optional attachments do not require acknowledgment; a required child column alone does not require a child row. Type-required media is already validated at each executed write. A direct non-null assertion also enforces media at that write; neither requires a skipped child row. Unknown helper returns and nullable expression alternatives remain advisory/test coverage, without an inferred argument-omission gate. Review an intentional exception with an individual source-bound disposition.

### N_CREATION_REASON

N_CREATION_REASON: Creating a row accepts a reason argument without a positive non-whitespace minimum. Compare the justification requirement with the spec; use text 1..N when required. This name-based question is advisory.

### N_CREATION_MONEY

N_CREATION_MONEY: Creating a row accepts omitted money. Model and require the amount when the spec requires a fee or payment; confirm intentional optional amounts against the spec. This name-based question is advisory.

### N_CONSTANT_APPROVAL

N_CONSTANT_APPROVAL: Creation stores a constant true approval/payment flag. A true constant does not verify payment or approval; record the required amount/evidence and the independently authorized decision. This name-based question is advisory.

### N_TRANSITION_BYPASS

N_TRANSITION_BYPASS: A generic enum setter reaches a state also reached by a stricter decision mutation. Apply the decision authority to every path reaching that state; write independent transition intent and run test.

### N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK

N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK: A creator checks a mutable eligibility column that an approval-like transition does not recheck. One question groups the created table, dependency and decision sites; immutable ownership reads and unrelated edits are excluded. Decide whether eligibility must remain current and write independent intent; test create, eligibility change, approval. The named literal-state fragment covers approved/accepted/authorized/granted/issued/active/confirmed/paid/signed and true approval flags; other synonyms are not inferred safe.

### N_DURATION_BOUNDS

N_DURATION_BOUNDS: Operations apply different server-relative duration bounds to one column. Compare the windows with the specification and make them consistent or document deliberate differences.

### N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE

N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE: A global role plus viewer attribution does not identify the owner of this resource. Ask whether any role member may write any row; model the target-to-owner relationship and guard it. See the Wall ownership rule recipe.

### E_SECURITY_NOTES

E_SECURITY_NOTES: the individual disposition file is malformed, cannot be written, or sources changed while recording a review. Inspect snapback/security-reviews.json and run check --json for current IDs. Supply a nonempty specification reason for one current note; neither bulk acknowledgments nor this file can clear an actual refutation.

### N_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY

N_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY: An argument-supplied column has a specific change guard on edit that creation does not imply. The question names that column, editor and guard, rather than a table-wide permission. Confirm creation authority against the specification; enforce the change guard at creation when required and test independent column write intent with non-null input. Exact changed-field comparisons in a stable single-write editor are supported. Target keys must be fixed inputs/IDs, and the editor cannot change its authority dependencies. Mixed conditions, changed branch aliases, mutable selectors, multiple target writes and early-return equivalents remain outside this heuristic, with generic insert inventory retained when applicable. Absence of advice is not a safety proof.

### N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY

N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY: A caller can initialize a private column without its change guard and outside its column read audience. This question ranks above ordinary asymmetry because the caller supplies private agency data it cannot read. Confirm who may supply this field at creation; enforce the guard when required and test independent column write intent with a non-null value. Read privacy alone does not define write authority; the question is advisory.

### N_EVIDENCE_REMOVAL

N_EVIDENCE_REMOVAL: Delete, detachment or explicit clearing can remove parent-linked media evidence. The question names removing operations and the parent reference, grouping the evidence columns. Required child fields alone do not require a surviving child; confirm last-child retention against the specification. Guard deletion or detachment transactionally, replace evidence or retire the parent, then declare independent parent integrity intent and run test. Ordinary non-null photo replacement is not removal; optional attachments without required creation linkage or independent integrity intent are excluded. This question is advisory.
