-- Food Truck Permits: city health department permits for food trucks.
--
-- Roles: operator (truck owners), inspector (field inspections and the live
-- board), supervisor (environmental health supervisor: suspends permits and
-- approves operators with a past suspension, recording the reason).
-- Role rows are provisioned by the local operator (service:operator), the
-- same pattern as `guide staging`: signup alone grants no role.
use identity password

table roster:
  principal: principal
  role: enum('operator', 'inspector', 'supervisor')
  unique principal
  immutable principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or viewer = 'service:operator'
  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  update <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  delete <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  online only

mutation provision(principal: principal, role: enum('operator', 'inspector', 'supervisor')):
  require viewer = 'service:operator' else NOT_OPERATOR
  return upsert roster[principal] { principal, role }

query myRole():
  return roster[viewer]

invariant roster.read only .principal = viewer or viewer = 'service:operator'
invariant roster.insert only viewer = 'service:operator'

-- Trucks belong to exactly one operator. activePermit points at the truck's
-- current permit once one is issued (maintained by approveApplication).
table trucks:
  owner: principal
  name: text 1..80
  licensePlate: text 1..16
  activePermit: permits ?
  unique licensePlate else PLATE_TAKEN
  immutable owner
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation registerTruck(name: text 1..80, licensePlate: text 1..16):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator'] else NOT_OPERATOR
  return insert trucks { owner: viewer, name, licensePlate }

query myTrucks(c: cursor ?):
  return trucks where .owner = viewer first 50 by .id after c

query allTrucks(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_STAFF
  return trucks first 50 by .id after c

invariant trucks.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
invariant trucks.insert only exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']

-- Each application must include interior photos, the fire extinguisher tag
-- photo and the permit fee: all three columns are required, so an
-- application row cannot exist without them.
table applications:
  truck: trucks
  owner: principal
  interiorPhotos: [image <=5000000] 1..4
  extinguisherTag: image <=5000000
  feeCents: int 1..100000000
  status: enum('applied', 'inspected', 'inspection recorded', 'approved', 'failed') default 'applied'
  failedAt: time default now
  lastPassed: bool default false
  createdAt: time default now
  immutable truck, owner, interiorPhotos, extinguisherTag, feeCents, createdAt
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation submitApplication(truck: trucks, interiorPhotos: [image <=5000000] 1..4, extinguisherTag: image <=5000000, feeCents: int 1..100000000):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator'] else NOT_OPERATOR
  t = trucks[truck] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require t.owner = viewer else NOT_YOUR_TRUCK
  flag = operatorFlags[viewer]
  require flag = null or flag.suspended = false else OPERATOR_SUSPENDED
  return insert applications { truck, owner: viewer, interiorPhotos, extinguisherTag, feeCents }

query myApplications(c: cursor ?):
  return applications where .owner = viewer first 50 by .createdAt after c

-- The live board: every application as it moves applied -> inspection recorded ->
-- approved / failed. Staff only.
query board(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_STAFF
  return applications first 50 by .createdAt after c

invariant applications.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
invariant applications.insert only exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']

-- v2 renamed the 'inspected' stage to 'inspection recorded'. The column still
-- admits the old value so that every historical row survives adoption and
-- rollback without dormancy; the migrateInspectionRecorded mutation below
-- moves every remaining 'inspected' row to the renamed stage (run after
-- deploy and after every forward), and no workflow mutation writes the old
-- value anymore. Rollback to a release without the renamed value projects
-- 'inspection recorded' back to 'inspected' instead of hiding rows.
down applications inspectedView(status: enum('applied', 'inspected', 'inspection recorded', 'approved', 'failed')): {status: 'inspected' if status = 'inspection recorded' else status}

-- One-shot data migration for the v2 rename. Supervisor-only. Moves a single
-- remaining 'inspected' application to 'inspection recorded'; callers page
-- the board until none remain.
mutation migrateInspectionRecorded(application: applications):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'inspected' else NOT_INSPECTED
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'inspection recorded' }
  return applications[app.id]

-- Inspections are written by inspectors (or supervisors). owner is the truck
-- owner's principal, denormalized so operators read their own reports and
-- staff read everything.
table inspections:
  truck: trucks
  application: applications ?
  permit: permits ?
  inspector: principal
  passed: bool
  notes: text <=2000 ?
  kind: enum('initial', 'reinspection', 'routine')
  inspectedAt: time default now
  owner: principal
  immutable truck, application, permit, inspector, passed, notes, kind, inspectedAt, owner
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

-- First inspection of an application: applied -> inspection recorded.
mutation inspectApplication(application: applications, passed: bool, notes: text <=2000 ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'applied' else NOT_APPLIED
  i = insert inspections { truck: app.truck, application: app.id, inspector: viewer, passed, notes, kind: 'initial', owner: app.owner }
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'inspection recorded', lastPassed: passed }
  return i

-- Reinspection of a failed application, only within ten days of the failure.
mutation reinspectApplication(application: applications, passed: bool, notes: text <=2000 ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'failed' else NOT_FAILED
  require now <= app.failedAt + 10d else REINSPECTION_LATE
  i = insert inspections { truck: app.truck, application: app.id, inspector: viewer, passed, notes, kind: 'reinspection', owner: app.owner }
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'inspection recorded', lastPassed: passed }
  return i

-- Decision on an inspection-recorded application. Approval issues the permit, valid
-- 365 days; rejection marks it failed and starts the ten-day reinspection
-- window.
mutation approveApplication(application: applications):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'inspection recorded' else NOT_INSPECTED
  require app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS
  p = insert permits { truck: app.truck, owner: app.owner, application: app.id, issuedAt: now, expiresAt: now + 365d, status: 'active', flagged: false, failedAt: now }
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'approved' }
  update trucks[app.truck] { activePermit: p.id }
  return p

mutation rejectApplication(application: applications):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'inspection recorded' else NOT_INSPECTED
  require not app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'failed', failedAt: now }
  return applications[app.id]

query myInspections(c: cursor ?):
  return inspections where .owner = viewer first 50 by .inspectedAt after c

query applicationInspections(application: applications, c: cursor ?):
  return inspections where .application = application first 50 by .inspectedAt after c

query permitInspections(permit: permits, c: cursor ?):
  return inspections where .permit = permit first 50 by .inspectedAt after c

invariant inspections.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
invariant inspections.insert only exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']

-- Permits are issued on approval and live 365 days. A failed routine
-- inspection flags the permit; a passing reinspection within ten days
-- clears the flag, otherwise the daily sweep suspends it.
table permits:
  truck: trucks
  owner: principal
  application: applications
  issuedAt: time default now
  expiresAt: time
  failedAt: time default now
  flagged: bool default false
  status: enum('active', 'suspended', 'expired') default 'active'
  immutable truck, owner, application, issuedAt, expiresAt
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

-- Routine inspection (or reinspection) of an active permit.
mutation inspectPermit(permit: permits, passed: bool, notes: text <=2000 ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  p = permits[permit] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require p.status = 'active' else PERMIT_NOT_ACTIVE
  require now < p.expiresAt else PERMIT_EXPIRED
  i = insert inspections { truck: p.truck, permit: p.id, inspector: viewer, passed, notes, kind: 'reinspection' if p.flagged else 'routine', owner: p.owner }
  update permits[p.id] { flagged: not passed, failedAt: now }
  return i

query myPermits(c: cursor ?):
  return permits where .owner = viewer first 50 by .issuedAt after c

query allPermits(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_STAFF
  return permits first 50 by .issuedAt after c

invariant permits.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']

-- Suspensions are written by the supervisor (recording the reason) or by
-- the daily sweep when the ten-day reinspection window lapses (reason
-- recorded, suspendedBy null for the automatic case).
table suspensions:
  permit: permits
  owner: principal
  reason: text 1..500
  suspendedBy: principal ?
  suspendedAt: time default now
  immutable permit, owner, reason, suspendedBy, suspendedAt
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation suspendPermit(permit: permits, reason: text 1..500):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  p = permits[permit] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require p.status = 'active' else PERMIT_NOT_ACTIVE
  require now < p.expiresAt else PERMIT_EXPIRED
  update permits[p.id] { status: 'suspended', flagged: false }
  upsert operatorFlags[p.owner] { operator: p.owner, suspended: true }
  return insert suspensions { permit: p.id, owner: p.owner, reason, suspendedBy: viewer }

query mySuspensions(c: cursor ?):
  return suspensions where .owner = viewer first 50 by .suspendedAt after c

invariant suspensions.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']

-- An operator whose permit was suspended cannot apply again until the
-- supervisor approves them, recording the reason.
table operatorFlags:
  operator: principal
  suspended: bool default true
  unique operator
  immutable operator
  read <- .operator = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

table approvals:
  operator: principal
  reason: text 1..500
  approvedBy: principal
  approvedAt: time default now
  immutable operator, reason, approvedBy, approvedAt
  read <- .operator = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation approveOperator(operator: principal, reason: text 1..500):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  flag = operatorFlags[operator] ! else OPERATOR_UNKNOWN
  require flag.suspended else OPERATOR_NOT_SUSPENDED
  update operatorFlags[operator] { suspended: false }
  return insert approvals { operator, reason, approvedBy: viewer }

query myFlag():
  return operatorFlags[viewer]

query myApprovals(c: cursor ?):
  return approvals where .operator = viewer first 50 by .approvedAt after c

query flaggedOperators(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  return operatorFlags where .suspended = true first 50 by .id after c

-- Daily sweep: suspend active flagged permits whose reinspection window
-- lapsed, and expire permits past their 365-day life.
job sweepPermits(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):
  for p in permits:
    if p.status = 'active' and p.flagged and p.failedAt < periodEnd - 10d:
      update permits[p.id] { status: 'suspended', flagged: false }
      insert suspensions { permit: p.id, owner: p.owner, reason: 'No passing reinspection within ten days of the failed inspection.', suspendedAt: periodEnd }
      upsert operatorFlags[p.owner] { operator: p.owner, suspended: true }
    if p.status = 'active' and p.expiresAt <= periodEnd:
      update permits[p.id] { status: 'expired', flagged: false }

schedule dailySweep every 1d at 03:00: sweepPermits
-- Mobile Notary Signings.
-- Roles: owner (agency owner), dispatcher, notary, title_company.
-- A signing always carries the signer's ID scan and the signing fee.
-- Notaries read operational fields but never the fee or billing notes;
-- only dispatcher/owner read those. Title companies read only their own
-- signings. Status flows requested -> assigned -> signed -> returned, and
-- returned must land within 24h of signing. Only the owner approves a
-- notary for out-of-state papers.
use identity password
use personas alice, bob

-- Roster: exactly one role per principal. Only the local operator
-- (staging/production bootstrap) writes it; app code never self-assigns.
table roles:
  principal: principal
  role: enum('owner', 'dispatcher', 'notary', 'title_company')
  unique principal
  immutable principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher']
  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  update <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  delete <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  cap 1000
  online only

mutation provision(principal: principal, role: enum('owner', 'dispatcher', 'notary', 'title_company')):
  require viewer = 'service:operator' else NOT_OPERATOR
  return upsert roles[principal] { principal, role }

query myRole():
  return roles[viewer]

-- Out-of-state approval per notary. Notaries register themselves; only the
-- owner flips the approval flag.
table notaryProfiles:
  principal: principal
  approvedOutOfState: bool default false
  unique principal
  immutable principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher']
  insert <- .principal = viewer
  update <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner']
  cap 1000
  online only

mutation registerNotary():
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'notary'] else NOT_NOTARY
  if exists notaryProfiles[principal = viewer]:
    return notaryProfiles[principal = viewer]
  return insert notaryProfiles { principal: viewer }

mutation approveNotary(notary: principal, approved: bool):
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER 'Only the agency owner approves notaries for out-of-state papers.'
  require exists roles[principal = notary, role = 'notary'] else NOT_A_NOTARY 'That principal is not a notary.'
  p = notaryProfiles[principal = notary] ! else NOT_REGISTERED 'That notary has not registered.'
  update notaryProfiles[p.id] { approvedOutOfState: approved }
  return null

query notaryDirectory():
  return roles where .role = 'notary' first 100 by .principal

query approvalDirectory():
  return notaryProfiles first 100 by .principal

table signings:
  titleCompany: principal
  notary: principal ?
  signerName: text 1..120
  address: text 1..300
  documents: [text <=200] 1..20
  idScan: image <=10000000
  fee: money USD
  agencyBillingNotes: text <=2000 ? renamed from billingNotes
  outOfState: bool default false
  status: enum('requested', 'assigned', 'signed', 'returned') default 'requested'
  signedAt: time ?
  returnedAt: time ?
  createdAt: time default now
  immutable titleCompany, createdAt
  read <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'notary'] or .titleCompany = viewer
  read (fee, agencyBillingNotes) <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher']
  insert <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'title_company'] and .titleCompany = viewer
  update <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or .notary = viewer
  cap 5000
  online only

-- Title companies file signing requests for themselves. The ID scan and the
-- fee are required: omitting either refuses at decode time.
mutation createSigning(signerName: text 1..120, address: text 1..300, documents: [text <=200] 1..20, idScan: image <=10000000, fee: money USD, agencyBillingNotes: text <=2000 ? alias billingNotes, outOfState: bool ?):
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'title_company'] else NOT_TITLE_COMPANY
  return insert signings { titleCompany: viewer, signerName, address, documents, idScan, fee, agencyBillingNotes, outOfState: outOfState ?? false }

-- Dispatchers and the owner assign a registered notary. Out-of-state papers
-- need an owner-approved notary.
mutation assignSigning(signing: signings, notary: principal):
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_STAFF 'Only dispatch staff assign signings.'
  require exists roles[principal = notary, role = 'notary'] else NOT_A_NOTARY 'That principal is not a notary.'
  s = signings[signing] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require s.status = 'requested' else BAD_STATUS 'Only requested signings can be assigned.'
  if s.outOfState:
    p = notaryProfiles[principal = notary] ! else NOT_APPROVED 'That notary is not approved for out-of-state papers.'
    require p.approvedOutOfState else NOT_APPROVED 'That notary is not approved for out-of-state papers.'
  update signings[signing] { notary, status: 'assigned' }
  return null

-- The assigned notary (or dispatch staff) records the signing.
mutation markSigned(signing: signings):
  s = signings[signing] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require s.status = 'assigned' else BAD_STATUS 'Only assigned signings can be marked signed.'
  require s.notary = viewer or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_ASSIGNED 'Only the assigned notary or dispatch staff records the signing.'
  update signings[signing] { status: 'signed', signedAt: now }
  return null

-- Signed papers ship back within one day of signing; later returns refuse.
mutation markReturned(signing: signings):
  s = signings[signing] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require s.status = 'signed' else BAD_STATUS 'Only signed signings can be marked returned.'
  require s.signedAt != null else BAD_STATUS 'This signing has no signing time.'
  require now <= s.signedAt + 24h else RETURN_OVERDUE 'Signed papers must ship back within one day of signing.'
  require s.notary = viewer or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_ASSIGNED 'Only the assigned notary or dispatch staff records the return.'
  update signings[signing] { status: 'returned', returnedAt: now }
  return null

-- Dispatch staff adjust the fee charged to the title company. Only the
-- agency owner may change the agency billing notes; the dispatcher may
-- still read them but cannot change them.
mutation updateBilling(signing: signings, fee: money USD, agencyBillingNotes: text <=2000 ? alias billingNotes):
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_STAFF 'Only dispatch staff edits billing.'
  s = signings[signing] ! else NOT_FOUND
  if agencyBillingNotes != s.agencyBillingNotes:
    require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER_NOTES 'Only the agency owner changes agency billing notes.'
  update signings[signing] { fee, agencyBillingNotes }
  return null

-- Live dispatch board: every caller sees exactly the rows (and columns)
-- their rules admit, newest arrivals last.
query board(c: cursor ?):
  return signings first 100 by .id after c

query signingDetail(signing: signings):
  return signings[signing]

-- Privacy intent, stated independently from the rules above.
invariant signings.read only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'notary'] or .titleCompany = viewer
invariant signings.fee read only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher']
invariant signings.agencyBillingNotes read only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher']
invariant signings.updateBilling only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher']
invariant signings.insert only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'title_company']
invariant notaryProfiles.approveNotary only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner']
invariant notaryProfiles.approvedOutOfState update only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner']
invariant signings.assignSigning only exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner']

//! Narrow stable leading-require reduction for operation-specific authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown write sets never establish stability.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Stmt,ProgramDecl,ProgramKind,InvariantDecl,RuleDecl};
use super::super::catalog::TableInfo;
pub(super) fn resolve<'a>(catalog:&'a Catalog,decl:&InvariantDecl)->Option<(String,&'a ProgramDecl)>{
    if matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"always"|"write"){return None;}
    let matches=catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|{let module=std::path::Path::new(&d.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();d.programs.iter().map(move|p|(super::super::operation_name(&module,&p.name),p))})
        .filter(|(name,p)|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(name==&decl.target||p.name==decl.target)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if matches.len()==1 {matches.into_iter().next()}else{None}
}
pub(super) fn safe(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {match rule{
    RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}=>key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(safe),
    RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>[a,b].iter().all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>true,_=>false,
}}
pub(super) fn requirements(catalog:&Catalog,info:&TableInfo,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<RuleExpr>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){let Stmt::Require{condition,site,..}=s else{break};
        let decl=RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:condition.clone(),site:site.clone()};
        if let Ok(rule)=super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&decl)&&safe(&rule){out.push(rule);}
    }out
}
pub(super) fn prove(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<bool,String>{
    let p=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|Some(&p.program.name)==inv.operation.as_ref()).ok_or("operation did not bind")?;
    if !safe(inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("unsupported policy")?){return Err("operation policy needs unsupported target-image, negative-probe or live-reference bindings".into());}
    let writes=snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program);
    if !writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table){return Err("operation has no direct write to the named table; implicit effects require a reduction".into());}
    let dependencies=inv.requirements.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|dependencies.contains(t)) {return Err("require dependency can be changed by this operation, including its target write".into());}
    if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.maintained.is_empty()||!t.sequences.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())) || !bound.schema.maintains.is_empty(){return Err("implicit maintain/sequence/referential writes require dependency stability reduction".into());}
    // Source helpers/control flow may hide write sets or viewer changes.
    let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool{match v{serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"For"|"If"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"|"Emit"))||m.values().any(opaque),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false}}
    if opaque(&json){return Err("operation control flow or opaque effects are outside stable leading-require reduction".into());}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(inv.requirements.clone());
    // Table-rule gates can only further restrict a dominating stable require;
    // using the require alone overapproximates effective owner-field gates.
    kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())
}

fn deserialize_text_format<'de, D: serde::Deserializer<'de>>(deserializer: D) -> Result<Option<crate::schema::TextFormat>, D::Error> {
    match String::deserialize(deserializer)?.as_str() {
        "handle" => Ok(Some(crate::schema::TextFormat::Handle)),
        "url" => Ok(Some(crate::schema::TextFormat::Url)),
        "email" => Ok(Some(crate::schema::TextFormat::Email)),
        _ => Err(serde::de::Error::custom("text format must be handle, url, or email")),
    }
}

/// Every read site (point, scan, merge lane, recursion) in a program body,
/// with the tables it reads: what a live subscription and `check --cost`
/// both need.
pub fn read_sites(program: &Program) -> Vec<ReadSite> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    stmts_read_sites(&program.body, &mut out);
    out
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ReadSite {
    pub site: String,
    pub table: String,
    pub index: String,
    /// `point`, `scan`, or `walk`.
    pub kind: &'static str,
    pub take: u64,
}

fn stmts_read_sites(stmts: &[Stmt], out: &mut Vec<ReadSite>) {
    for stmt in stmts {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. } | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expr_read_sites(value, out),
            Stmt::Emit { payload: value, once, .. } => { expr_read_sites(value, out); if let Some(value) = once { expr_read_sites(value, out); } },
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => {
                expr_read_sites(condition, out);
                stmts_read_sites(then_body, out);
                stmts_read_sites(else_body, out);
            }
            Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
                match domain {
                    LoopDomain::Page(scan) => scan_read_sites(scan, out),
                    LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => { expr_read_sites(start, out); expr_read_sites(end_exclusive, out); }
                    LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expr_read_sites(value, out),
                    LoopDomain::Recurse(recurse) => {
                        expr_read_sites(&recurse.seed, out);
                        out.push(ReadSite { site: recurse.site.clone(), table: recurse.table.clone(), index: recurse.index.clone(), kind: "walk", take: recurse.depth as u64 * recurse.fanout.max(1) as u64 });
                    }
                    LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => {}
                }
                stmts_read_sites(body, out);
            }
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expr_read_sites(condition, out),
            Stmt::Check { condition, current } => {
                expr_read_sites(condition, out);
                out.push(ReadSite { site: current.site.clone(), table: current.table.clone(), index: current.index.clone(), kind: "point", take: 1 });
            }
            Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { row, .. } | Stmt::Insert { row, .. } => expr_read_sites(row, out),
            Stmt::Update { key, patch, .. } => {
                expr_read_sites(key, out);
                expr_read_sites(patch, out);
            }
            Stmt::Upsert { key, row, .. } => {
                expr_read_sites(key, out);
                expr_read_sites(row, out);
            }
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, patch, condition, .. } => {
                out.push(ReadSite { site: target.site.clone(), table: target.table.clone(), index: target.index.clone(), kind: "write target", take: 1 });
                for key in &target.key { expr_read_sites(key, out); }
                expr_read_sites(patch, out);
                if let Some(condition) = condition { stmts_read_sites(&condition.setup, out); expr_read_sites(&condition.value, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Delete { key, .. } => expr_read_sites(key, out),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => {
                out.push(ReadSite { site: target.site.clone(), table: target.table.clone(), index: target.index.clone(), kind: "write target", take: 1 });
                for key in &target.key { expr_read_sites(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => {}
        }
    }
}

fn scan_read_sites(scan: &Scan, out: &mut Vec<ReadSite>) {
    if let Some(filter) = &scan.filter { expr_read_sites(filter, out); }
    if let Some(range) = &scan.range {
        for bound in [&range.gt, &range.gte, &range.lt, &range.lte].into_iter().flatten() { expr_read_sites(bound, out); }
    }
    for key in &scan.prefix {
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — matching expressions can read independent sources; retain them in the execution facts.
        match key { IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(expr) | IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(expr) => expr_read_sites(expr, out) }
    }
    for cursor in [&scan.after, &scan.before].into_iter().flatten() { expr_read_sites(cursor, out); }
    out.push(ReadSite { site: scan.site.clone(), table: scan.table.clone(), index: scan.index.clone(), kind: "scan", take: scan.take.maximum() });
}

fn expr_read_sites(expr: &Expr, out: &mut Vec<ReadSite>) {
    match expr {
        Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Variable(_) | Expr::Input(_) => {}
        Expr::Field { base, .. } => expr_read_sites(base, out),
        Expr::Array(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| expr_read_sites(item, out)),
        Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_read_sites(item, out)),
        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => { expr_read_sites(base, out); fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_read_sites(item, out)); }
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            expr_read_sites(left, out);
            expr_read_sites(right, out);
        }
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } => expr_read_sites(value, out),
        Expr::Builtin { args, .. } => args.iter().for_each(|item| expr_read_sites(item, out)),
        Expr::Get(read) | Expr::Exists(read) => {
            read.key.iter().for_each(|item| expr_read_sites(item, out));
            out.push(ReadSite { site: read.site.clone(), table: read.table.clone(), index: read.index.clone(), kind: "point", take: 1 });
        }
        Expr::Block { body, value } => { stmts_read_sites(body, out); expr_read_sites(value, out); }
        Expr::Scan(scan) => scan_read_sites(scan, out),
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            if let Some(after) = merge.position.as_ref().and_then(|position| position.after.as_ref()) { expr_read_sites(after, out); }
            for lane in &merge.lanes {
                if let Some(guard) = &lane.guard { expr_read_sites(guard, out); }
                if let Some(project) = &lane.project { expr_read_sites(project, out); }
                if let Some(value) = &lane.distinct { expr_read_sites(value, out); }
                stmts_read_sites(&lane.setup, out);
                if let Some(source) = &lane.source {
                    if let Some(scan) = source.scan() { scan_read_sites(scan, out); }
                    for value in source.expressions() { expr_read_sites(value, out); }
                }
                scan_read_sites(&lane.scan, out);
            }
        }
    }
}

        }
        for (field, capture) in &table.recorded {
            let target = schema.table(&capture.table);
            let valid = !table.defaults.contains_key(field) && field == &format!("{}.{}", capture.reference, capture.column)
                && !capture.reference.contains('.') && !capture.column.contains('.')
                && table.columns.get(&capture.reference).is_some_and(|kind| kind.required() == &ColumnType::Ref { table: capture.table.clone() })
                && table.immutable_fields().any(|name| name == capture.reference)
                && target.is_some_and(|target| target.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable()
                    && (capture.column == "id" || target.immutable_fields().any(|name| name == capture.column))
                    && target.columns.get(&capture.column).is_some_and(|kind| table.columns.get(field) == Some(&ColumnType::Optional(Box::new(kind.required().clone())))));
            if !valid { issue(&mut issues, format!("{path}.recorded.{field}"), "recorded attribution requires one immutable reference to a public target's immutable column or id, with an optional stored type; guide maintains".into()); }
        }
        if table.columns.get("id") != Some(&ColumnType::Id) {
            issue(&mut issues, format!("{path}.columns.id"), "every table has an intrinsic id column".into());
        }
        match table.indexes.get(BY_ID) {
            Some(index) if index.is_plain() && index.unique && index.columns() == ["id"] => {}
            _ => issue(&mut issues, format!("{path}.indexes.{BY_ID}"), "every table has the intrinsic unique byId index".into()),
        }
        for (name, index) in &table.indexes {
            let ipath = format!("{path}.indexes.{name}");
            if index.components.is_empty() || index.components.len() > 8 {
                issue(&mut issues, ipath.clone(), "declare between 1 and 8 components".into());
            }
            let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
            let mut words = 0;
            };
            let key = ast::KeyPart { column: None, value: A::Name { name: table.clone(), site: site.clone() }, site: site.clone() };
            let resolved = A::Write { op: *op, table: target, keys: vec![key], fields: fields.clone(), if_absent: *if_absent, condition: condition.clone(), site: site.clone() };
            return self.write_inner(&resolved, out, valued);
        }
        if self.in_prelude {
            return Err(placement(site, "a write belongs to a chunk", format!("move {} {table} inside a top-level `for row in table:` job sweep", op.word())));
        }
        if self.kind == ProgramKind::Query {
            return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_PLACEMENT", format!("queries cannot {} {table}", op.word()), "move the write into a mutation").guide("programs"));
        }
        let info = self.table(table, site)?;
        if info.maintained.is_some() {
            return Err(unknown(site, format!("{table} is a maintained aggregate; write its source rows instead"), format!("{} {}", op.word(), info.maintained.as_ref().map(|m| m.source.clone()).unwrap_or_default())));
        }
        for field in fields {
            if info.decl.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == field.name && c.sequence.is_some()) {
                return Err(Diagnostic::new(&field.site, "E_SEQUENCE_SUPPLIED", format!("{table}.{} is assigned by the engine", field.name),
                    format!("omit {} from the {} payload", field.name, op.word())).guide("writes"));
            }
            if super::columns::fields(info).any(|name|name == field.name) {
                return Err(super::columns::refusal(&field.site,format!("{table}.{} is engine-maintained; write its source rows instead",field.name)));
            }
        }
        self.system_principal_write(info, op, fields, *if_absent, site)?;
        self.note_denied_write(info, op, fields, site);
        // Writes share table-level residency facts, but every source occurrence
        // (including an expanded helper) belongs in a changed-prediction note.
        let write_name = format!("{} {table}", op.word());
        self.sources.insert(format!("write/{write_name}/{site}"), (site.clone(), write_name));
        match op {
            WriteOp::Insert => {
                let (row, row_ty) = self.write_row(info, fields, None, site, out)?;
                let row_ty = if self.schema.tables[table].column_reads.is_empty() || self.ordinary_write_lifetime(table) && self.row_independent_read_proved(table) { row_ty } else { row_ty.optional() };
                let tmp = self.temp("inserted");
                out.push(Stmt::Const { name: tmp.clone(), value: row });
                if *if_absent {
                    let result = self.temp("absent");
                    out.push(Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { table: table.clone(), row: ir::var(&tmp), result: result.clone() });
                    return Ok(valued.then(|| (self.project_insert(info, ir::var(&result)), row_ty.optional())));
                }
                out.push(Stmt::Insert { table: table.clone(), row: ir::var(&tmp) });
                Ok(valued.then(|| (self.project_insert(info, ir::var(&tmp)), row_ty)))
            }
            WriteOp::Update | WriteOp::Upsert => {
                // @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — upsert's insert arm has no old row on which to decide a condition.
                if *op == WriteOp::Upsert && condition.is_some() {
                    return Err(unknown(site, "upsert cannot take a condition", "a condition belongs on update; an upsert inserts or patches"));
                }
                // @ref LLP 3000.007#8 — item 7: target resolution and conditional old images belong to the engine.
                let key = self.write_key(info, keys, site, out).map_err(|error| self.extra_write_test(info, expr, error))?;
                let upsert = *op == WriteOp::Upsert;
                if upsert && fields.iter().any(|f| f.name == "id") {
                    return Err(unknown(site, "upsert cannot spell id; the key names the row", "remove id from the patch; the key names the row"));
                }
                let (patch, _) = self.write_row(info, fields, Some("patch"), site, out)?;
                self.immutable_patch(info, &key, &patch, fields, site, out, upsert, condition.is_some())?;
                let condition = if let Some(condition) = condition {
                    let old = self.temp("condition");
                    let prior = self.focal.replace((old.clone(), Ty::Row(table.clone())));
                    let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
                    let bound = self.expr(condition, &mut setup);
                    self.focal = prior;
                    let (value, ty) = bound?;
                    if !ty.is_bool() {
                        return Err(type_error(condition.site(), format!("update's condition must be bool, got {}", ty.name()), "update t[key] { body } if .body = expected"));
                    }
                    out.nest(&format!("{}.condition", out.len()), &setup.ownership);
                    Some(ir::WriteCondition { old, setup: setup.code, value })
                } else { None };
                let result = valued.then(|| self.temp("written"));
                let target = Read { site: site.to_string(), table: table.clone(), index: key.index, key: key.key, provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape };
                out.push(Stmt::WriteTarget { target, patch, upsert, condition, result: result.clone() });
                let row_ty = self.write_result_type(info);
                let row_ty = if self.ordinary_write_lifetime(table) && self.row_independent_read_proved(table) {
                    row_ty
                } else { row_ty.optional() };
                Ok(result.map(|name| (ir::var(&name), row_ty)))
            }
            WriteOp::Delete => {
                let key = self.write_key(info, keys, site, out).map_err(|error| self.extra_write_test(info, expr, error))?;
                if key.alternate {
                    let location = self.site();
                    self.sources.insert(location.clone(), (site.clone(), format!("delete {table}")));
                    out.push(Stmt::DeleteTarget { target: Read { site: location, table: table.clone(), index: key.index, key: key.key, provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape } });
                } else {
                    out.push(Stmt::Delete { table: table.clone(), key: key.key[0].clone() });
                }
                Ok(valued.then(|| (Expr::Literal(Value::Null), Ty::Null)))
            }
        }
    }

    /// `t[row]` where `row` is a present row of `t`: the same read as `t[row.id]`.
    fn row_as_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<ReadExpr> {
        if read.keys.len() != 1 || read.keys[0].column.is_some() || read.range.is_some() || read.filter.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() || read.index.is_some() || !read.order_keys.is_empty() { return None; }
        let key = &read.keys[0].value;
        if !matches!(key, A::Name { .. }) { return None; }
        let (_, ty) = self.clone().expr(key, &mut setup::Body::default()).ok()?;
        if !matches!(ty, Ty::Row(ref table) | Ty::WriteRow(ref table) if *table == read.table) { return None; }
        let mut by_id = read.clone();
        by_id.keys[0].value = A::Member { base: Box::new(key.clone()), name: "id".into(), site: key.site().clone() };
        Some(by_id)
    }

    fn write_key(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, keys: &[ast::KeyPart], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<WriteKey> {
        if keys.is_empty() {
            return Err(unknown(site, format!("{} needs a key: which row?", info.decl.name), format!("{}[id]", info.decl.name)));
        }
        // A row value as the key addresses the row by its id.
        if keys.len() == 1 && keys[0].column.is_none() {
            let mut row_setup = setup::Body::default();
            let (e, t) = self.expr(&keys[0].value, &mut row_setup)?;
            if let Ty::Row(table) | Ty::WriteRow(table) = t.required()
                && table == info.decl.name
            {
                out.extend(row_setup);
                let id = self.temp("key");
                out.push(Stmt::Let { name: id.clone(), value: ir::field(e, "id") });
                return Ok(WriteKey { index: "byId".into(), key: vec![ir::var(&id)], alternate: false });
            }
        }
        let read = ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), table: info.decl.name.clone(), keys: keys.to_vec(), index: None, range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), site: site.clone() };
        let (bound, index, _) = self.select_index(info, &read, ReadMode::Point, out)?;
        super::point_key::check(info, &index, &read, &bound.iter().map(|(column, _, ty)|
            (column.as_deref(), !ty.is_optional() && *ty != Ty::Unknown)).collect::<Vec<_>>(), false)?;
        let key = self.order_keys(info, &index, bound)?;
        if index.name == "byId" {
            let id = self.temp("key");
            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: id.clone(), value: key[0].clone() });
            return Ok(WriteKey { index: "byId".into(), key: vec![ir::var(&id)], alternate: false });
        }
        Ok(WriteKey { index: index.name, key, alternate: true })
    }

    fn write_row(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, fields: &[ObjectField], current: Option<&str>, site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        objects::explicit_fields(fields.iter().filter(|f| !f.spread).map(|f| (f.name.as_str(), &f.site)))?;
        let expanded;
        let fields = if fields.iter().any(|field| field.spread) {
            expanded = self.expand_object_fields(fields, Some(info), out)?;
            &expanded
        } else { fields };
        for field in fields {
            if super::columns::fields(info).any(|name|name == field.name) {
                return Err(super::columns::refusal(&field.site,format!("{}.{} is engine-maintained; write its source rows instead",info.decl.name,field.name)));
            }
        }
        match current {
            None => {
                if fields.iter().any(|f| f.name == "id") {
                    let (given, ty) = fields.iter().find(|f| f.name == "id").map(|f| f.value.as_ref().map(|v| self.expr_as_reference(v, out, &Ty::Id)).unwrap_or_else(|| self.name("id", site))).unwrap()?;
//! Explain residual write tests without lowering them to a private pre-read.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    pub(super) fn extra_write_test(&self, info: &'a TableInfo, write: &A, original: Diagnostic) -> Diagnostic {
        let A::Write { table, keys, site, .. } = write else { return original };
        let read = ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), table: table.clone(),
            keys: keys.clone(), index: None, range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), site: site.clone() };
        let Ok(read) = super::super::mixed_keys::read(info, &read, self.catalog) else { return original };
        let read = read.into_owned();
        let keys = &read.keys;
        // Speculate only for an explanation. Mistyped, ambiguous or incomplete
        // keys keep their original error; no setup escapes into the program.
        let mut probe = self.clone();
        if probe.read_value(&read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint, &mut setup::Body::default()).is_err() { return original; }
        let Ok((_, index, _)) = probe.select_index(info, &read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint, &mut setup::Body::default()) else { return original };
        if keys.len() <= index.columns.len() { return original; }
        let positional = !self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(table.clone(), index.name.clone()))
            && info.indexes.iter().filter(|candidate| candidate.unique && candidate.columns.len() == index.columns.len()
                && !self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(table.clone(), candidate.name.clone()))).count() == 1;
        let key: Vec<_> = index.columns.iter().map(|column| {
            let mut part = keys.iter().find(|part| part.column.as_ref() == Some(column)).expect("validated named key").clone();
            if positional { part.column = None; }
            part
        }).collect();
        let mut checked = read.clone();
        checked.keys = key.clone();
        checked.keys.extend(keys.iter().filter(|part| !index.columns.contains(part.column.as_ref().expect("named key"))).cloned());
        let mut fixed = write.clone();
        let A::Write { keys, .. } = &mut fixed else { unreachable!() };
        *keys = key;
        Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_WRITE_TESTS", "a write target names one row by its key; extra column tests belong in an explicit require",
            format!("require exists {} else NOT_ADMIN\n{}", print::read_expr(&checked), print::expression(&fixed))).guide("writes")
    }
}
//! Seeks and row-local scan predicates.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.011.000.001#4 — retain the binder's actual choice for why, never infer a new lookup or rewrite.
    pub(super) fn record_selection(&mut self, site: &str, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, result: &str, reason: &str) {
        let named = read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some());
        let bindings = index.columns.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(position, column)| {
            let key = if named { read.keys.iter().find(|key| key.column.as_ref() == Some(column)) } else { read.keys.get(position) }?;
            Some(format!("{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value)))
        }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        let origin = if self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())) { "synthesized; " } else { "" };
        let source = if origin.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!("; read: {}", self.catalog.read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| print::read_expr(read))) };
        let mut selection = format!("selected {} ({bindings}); result: {result}; {origin}{reason}{source}", index.name);
        if !self.variants.is_empty() {
            let when = self.variants.iter().map(|(name, present)| format!("{} {}", name.trim_start_matches("$intentPresent:").trim_start_matches("$intentSearch:"), if *present { "present" } else { "absent" })).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
            selection = format!("when {when}: {selection}");
        }
        self.selections.entry(site.into()).and_modify(|prior| { if !prior.contains(&selection) { prior.push_str("; "); prior.push_str(&selection); } }).or_insert(selection);
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#10 — a covered total unique key reads one row; additional named equalities check that row.
    pub(super) fn read_value(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Expr> {
        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
        for key in &read.keys { if let Some(field) = &key.column { super::super::row_views::predicate_field(info, field, &read.site)?; } }
        if !read.seeks.is_empty() { return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek needs a bounded page", "t[prefix, col >= start] first 50 by index")); }
        let mut point = read.clone();
        if point.keys.is_empty() && info.maintained.as_ref().is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) {
            point.keys.push(ast::KeyPart { column: None, value: A::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(true), site: read.site.clone() }, site: read.site.clone() });
            point.index = Some("byGroup".into());
        } else if point.keys.is_empty() {
            return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{}[] reads nothing; a point read needs a key", read.table), format!("{}[id]", read.table)));
        }
        let implicit_group = mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_some() && point.index.is_none();
        if implicit_group { point.index = Some("byGroup".into()); }
        let (mut keys, index, mut selection) = self.select_index(info, &point, mode, out)?;
        super::super::point_key::check(info, &index, read, &keys.iter().map(|(column, _, ty)|
            (column.as_deref(), !ty.is_optional() && *ty != Ty::Unknown)).collect::<Vec<_>>(), false)?;
        if implicit_group { selection = "complete maintained group key".into(); }
        let extra = keys.len() > index.columns.len();
        let mut equalities = Vec::new();
        if extra {
            for (column, value, ty) in &mut keys {
                let column = column.as_ref().expect("named key");
                if column != "id" && !info.columns.contains_key(column) {
                    return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no column {column}", read.table), name_rewrite(column, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)));
                }
                let expected = self.key_ty(info, &index, column);
                if !ty.comparable(&expected) { return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", ty.name(), read.table, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name()))); }
                *value = self.snapshot(value.clone(), out);
                if !index.columns.contains(column) { equalities.push((column.clone(), value.clone())); }
            }
            keys.retain(|(column, _, _)| index.columns.contains(column.as_ref().unwrap()));
        }
        let key = self.order_keys(info, &index, keys)?;
        let location = self.site();
        self.record_selection(&location, read, &index, if mode == ReadMode::Exists { "exists" } else if info.maintained.is_some() { "scalar" } else { "row or null" }, &selection);
        self.sources.insert(location.clone(), (read.site.clone(), print::read_expr(read)));
        let mut point = Read { site: location, table: read.table.clone(), index: index.name, key, provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape };
        if mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_none() {
            self.optional_lookup_note(read, &point);
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.028#3.4 — only a caller-proved existence read may use the whole owner partition.
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && equalities.is_empty() {
            super::super::own_keys::existence(self.schema, &mut point);
        }
        if equalities.is_empty() { return Ok(if mode == ReadMode::Exists { Expr::Exists(point) } else { Expr::Get(point) }); }
        let row = self.temp("probe");
        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Get(point) });
        let mut condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::var(&row)), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5-what-derives-from-the-capture — a denied aggregate carries a null value, not a present group.
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && info.maintained.is_some() {
            let present = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), "value")), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(present) };
        }
        for (column, value) in equalities {
            let test = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), &column)), right: Box::new(value) };
            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(test) };
        }
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists { return Ok(condition); }
        out.push(Stmt::If { condition, then_body: Vec::new(), else_body: vec![Stmt::Assign { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) }] });
        Ok(ir::var(&row))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.3 — a seek constrains the first index column after the pinned prefix.
    pub(super) fn seek(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<ir::Range>> {
        if read.seeks.is_empty() { return Ok(None); }
        let mut range = ir::Range { gt: None, gte: None, lt: None, lte: None };

            if index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) != Some(&bound.column) || read.matching.is_some() {
                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek compares the first unpinned column of a plain index", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
            }
            let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column);
            let (value, ty) = self.field_value(&ObjectField { spread: false, name: bound.column.clone(), value: Some(bound.value.clone()), site: bound.site.clone() }, &expected, out)?;
            if !ty.comparable(&expected) || ty.is_optional() {
                let endpoint = print::expression(&bound.value);
                let rewrite = if ty.is_optional() {
                    format!("put the read inside `if {endpoint} != null:`, then `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
                } else {
                    format!("use a present {} endpoint in `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", expected.required().name(), read.table, bound.column, bound.op)

                for field in fields {
                    if field.name == "id" {
                        return Err(unknown(&field.site, "update cannot change id", "remove id from the patch"));
                    }
                    let Some(scalar) = info.columns.get(&field.name) else {
                        return Err(unknown(&field.site, format!("{} has no column {}", info.decl.name, field.name), name_rewrite(&field.name, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
                    };
                    let expected = Ty::from_scalar(scalar);
                    let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
                    let (e, t) = self.field_value(field, &expected, &mut setup)?;
                    literal_in_range(info, &field.name, scalar, &e, &field.site)?;
                    self.sequence_setup(exprs.iter_mut().map(|(_, e)| e), setup, out);
                    if !t.writable_to(&expected) {
                        return Err(diagnostics::write_field_type(info, field, &expected, &t));
                    }
                    let e = if expr::allocates(&e) { self.snapshot(e, out) } else { e };
                    exprs.push((field.name.clone(), e));
                    if types.insert(field.name.clone(), t).is_some() {
                        return Err(unknown(&field.site, format!("field {} is written twice", field.name), "keep one"));
                    }
                }
                Ok((Expr::Object(exprs.into_iter().collect()), Ty::Object(types)))
            }
        }
    }

    fn write_row_with_id(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, fields: &[ObjectField], id: Expr, site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        let id = self.snapshot(id, out);
        let mut exprs = vec![("id".to_owned(), id)];
        let mut given = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        for field in fields {
            let Some(scalar) = info.columns.get(&field.name) else {
                return Err(unknown(&field.site, format!("{} has no column {}", info.decl.name, field.name), name_rewrite(&field.name, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
            };
            let expected = Ty::from_scalar(scalar);
            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
            let (e, t) = self.insert_field_value(info, field, &expected, &mut setup)?;
            literal_in_range(info, &field.name, scalar, &e, &field.site)?;
            self.sequence_setup(exprs.iter_mut().map(|(_, e)| e), setup, out);
            if !t.writable_to(&expected) {
                return Err(diagnostics::write_field_type(info, field, &expected, &t));
            }
            let e = if expr::allocates(&e) { self.snapshot(e, out) } else { e };
            if !given.insert(field.name.clone()) {
                return Err(unknown(&field.site, format!("field {} is written twice", field.name), "keep one"));
            }
            exprs.push((field.name.clone(), e));
        }
        // Materialize the same defaults in the insert expression so its returned row is the written image.
        for column in &info.decl.columns {
            if !given.contains(&column.name) && let Some(default) = &column.default {
                if column.written_by.is_some() || self.schema.receipt_assigned(&info.decl.name, &column.name) {
                    // Preserve omission until the engine checks supplied-field authority.
                    given.insert(column.name.clone());
                    continue;
                }
                let default = super::defaults::column(column, default)?;
                let value = match default {
                    snapback4_core::schema::ColumnDefault::Literal(value) => Expr::Literal(value),
                    snapback4_core::schema::ColumnDefault::Now => Expr::Input(Input::Now),
                    snapback4_core::schema::ColumnDefault::Viewer => Expr::Input(Input::Viewer),
                };
                exprs.push((column.name.clone(), value));
                given.insert(column.name.clone());
            }
        }
        let missing: Vec<&str> = info.columns.iter().filter(|(name, scalar)| !scalar.optional && !self.schema.receipt_assigned(&info.decl.name, name) && !info.decl.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == **name && c.sequence.is_some()) && !super::columns::fields(info).any(|f|f == *name) && !given.contains(*name)).map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
        if !missing.is_empty() {
            return Err(type_error(site, format!("insert into {} omits required column{} {}", info.decl.name, if missing.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, missing.join(", ")), format!("insert {} {{ {} }}", info.decl.name, given.iter().cloned().chain(missing.iter().map(|m| format!("{m}: <{}>", info.columns.get(*m).map(print::scalar_type).unwrap_or_default()))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
        }
        Ok((Expr::Object(exprs.into_iter().collect()), self.write_result_type(info)))
    }

    fn project_insert(&self, info: &TableInfo, row: Expr) -> Expr {
        if info.decl.columns.iter().any(|column| column.written_by.is_some()) || !self.schema.tables[&info.decl.name].column_reads.is_empty() {
            Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::ProjectFields { table: info.decl.name.clone() }, args: vec![row] }
        } else { row }
    }

    fn write_result_type(&self, info: &TableInfo) -> Ty {
        if super::columns::fields(info).next().is_some() { Ty::WriteRow(info.decl.name.clone()) } else { Ty::Row(info.decl.name.clone()) }
    }

    fn calendar_number(&self, value: &A, depth: u8) -> bool {
        if depth > 8 { return false; }
        match value {
            A::Call { name, .. } => matches!(name.as_str(), "day" | "hour" | "bucket"),
            A::Name { name, .. } => self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| binding.revision == 0 && binding.origins.len() == 1 && self.calendar_number(&binding.origins[0].1, depth + 1)),
            A::If { then, otherwise, .. } => self.calendar_number(then, depth + 1) || self.calendar_number(otherwise, depth + 1),
            _ => false,
        }
    }

    fn field_value(&mut self, field: &ObjectField, expected: &Ty, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        if expected.required() == Ty::Time && field.value.as_ref().map_or_else(
            || self.calendar_number(&A::Name { name: field.name.clone(), site: field.site.clone() }, 0), |value| self.calendar_number(value, 0)) {
            return Err(type_error(&field.site, "day/hour/bucket returns a period number, not a time in milliseconds",
                "store the period number in an int field, or use day(now) * 1d for a UTC midnight time (hour(now) * 1h for an hour)"));
        }
        match &field.value {
            Some(value) if expected.required().is_identity() => self.expr_as_reference(value, out, expected),
68:    /// Field selections and an optional row base reach the existing IR. Reads
103:                        let key = if ty.is_optional() { format!("{expression} ! else NOT_FOUND") } else { expression };
110:                if ty.is_optional() {

        let name = text[at + 1..].bytes().take_while(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || *b == b'_').count();
        name > 0 && bytes.get(at + 1 + name) == Some(&b'.')
    })
}

fn operation_name(module: &str, name: &str) -> String {
    if module == "schema" { name.to_owned() } else { format!("{module}.{name}") }
}

/// Programs and rules use the same i53 literal domain. A token must never
/// change its numeric meaning because it was written in a rule position.
fn integer_literal(text: &str, site: &crate::diag::Site) -> crate::diag::Fallible<i64> {
    text.parse::<i64>().ok().filter(|n| (snapback4_core::value::I53_MIN..=snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX).contains(n)).ok_or_else(|| crate::diag::type_error(site, "number literals are integers within ±(2^53 − 1)", "use a decimal column with scaled integer minor units"))
}
//! Mutable syntax traversal shared by planning and final index-name resolution.
use crate::ast::*;

pub(crate) fn expression(value: &mut Expr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    match value {
        Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } => read_children(read, visit),
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } | Expr::Member { base: value, .. } | Expr::Assert { value, .. } | Expr::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => { expression(left, visit); expression(right, visit); }
        Expr::Array { items, .. } | Expr::Call { args: items, .. } => { for item in items { expression(item, visit); } }
        Expr::Object { fields, .. } => { for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } } }
        Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => { expression(base, visit); shape(fields, visit); }
        Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => { expression(then, visit); expression(condition, visit); expression(otherwise, visit); }
        Expr::Walk { seed, .. } => expression(seed, visit),
        Expr::Write { keys, fields, condition, .. } => {
            for key in keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
            for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } }
            if let Some(value) = condition { expression(value, visit); }
        }
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            for value in [&mut merge.matching, &mut merge.filter, &mut merge.cursor].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
            let mut lanes = Vec::new();
            for mut lane in std::mem::take(&mut merge.lanes) {
                if let Some(predicate) = &mut lane.admission { expression(predicate, visit); }
                if let Some((_, read)) = &mut lane.nulls {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    let Expr::Read(planned) = value else { unreachable!("planned null scan") };
                    **read = planned;
                }
                if let Some(value) = &mut lane.distinct { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(fields) = &mut lane.project { shape(fields, visit); }
                for value in &mut lane.prefix { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some((_, value)) = &mut lane.loop_ { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(read) = &mut lane.planned {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    match value {
                        Expr::Read(planned) => **read = planned,
                        Expr::Merge(nested) if lane.loop_.is_none() => {
                            for mut child in nested.lanes {
                                child.branch = lane.branch.clone();
                                child.project = lane.project.clone();
                                lanes.push(child);
                            }
                            continue;
                        }
                        _ => unreachable!("a planned lane is a read or an expanded read"),
                    }
                }
                lanes.push(lane);
            }
            merge.lanes = lanes;
        }
        Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } => {}
    }
    visit(value);
}

fn read_children(read: &mut ReadExpr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for key in &mut read.keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
    for seek in &mut read.seeks { expression(&mut seek.value, visit); }
    for value in [&mut read.filter, &mut read.matching].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
    if let Some(value) = read.range.as_mut().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_mut()) { expression(value, visit); }
}

pub(super) fn shape(fields: &mut [ShapeField], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for field in fields { if let ShapeField::Field { value, .. } = field { expression(value, visit); } }
}

pub(super) fn statements(body: &mut [Stmt], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for stmt in body {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Bind { value, .. } | Stmt::Expr { value, .. } | Stmt::Return { value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expression(condition, visit),
            Stmt::Emit { payload, once, .. } => {
                expression(payload, visit);
                if let Some(key) = once { expression(key, visit); }
            }
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body, .. } => { expression(condition, visit); statements(then_body, visit); statements(else_body, visit); }
            Stmt::As { principal: domain, body, .. } | Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => { expression(domain, visit); statements(body, visit); }
            Stmt::Break { .. } | Stmt::Continue { .. } => {}
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn document(doc: &mut Document, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — historical lookups receive the same predicate/index planning as ordinary reads.
    for fill in doc.backfills.iter_mut().chain(&mut doc.downfills) {expression(&mut fill.value,visit);}

    for program in &mut doc.programs { statements(&mut program.prelude, visit); statements(&mut program.body, visit); }
    for function in &mut doc.functions {
        match &mut function.body { FunctionBody::Expression(value) => expression(value, visit), FunctionBody::Block(body) => statements(body, visit) }
    }
    for decl in &mut doc.shapes { shape(&mut decl.fields, visit); }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum ReadMode {
    /// A full unique key: one row.
    Point,
    /// A read may check extra equalities after a total unique lookup.
    FilteredPoint,
    /// A full key of any index: presence.
    Exists,
    /// A strict prefix: a page.
    Range,
}

    index: String,
    key: Vec<Expr>,
    alternate: bool,
}

impl Catalog {
    pub fn build(documents: Vec<Document>, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Self {
        let mut tables: BTreeMap<String, TableInfo> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut maintains = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut shapes: BTreeMap<String, ShapeDecl> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut functions: BTreeMap<String, FunctionDecl> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut effects = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut effect_sites: BTreeMap<String, Site> = BTreeMap::new();
        for doc in &documents {
            for table in &doc.tables {
                if tables.contains_key(&table.name) {
                    diagnostics.push(unknown(&table.site, format!("duplicate table {}", table.name), "rename one of the two tables"));
                    continue;
                }
                let mut columns = BTreeMap::new();
                let mut ok = true;
                for column in &table.columns {
                    if column.name == "id" {
                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&column.site, format!("table {} gets id implicitly; remove the id column", table.name), "delete this line"));
                        ok = false;
                        continue;
                    }
                    if columns.insert(column.name.clone(), column.type_.clone()).is_some() {
                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&column.site, format!("duplicate column {}.{}", table.name, column.name), "rename one of the two columns"));
                        ok = false;
                    }
                }
                for field in &table.search {
                    if !matches!(columns.get(field), Some(ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Text { .. } | TypeKind::Enum { .. }, .. })) {
                        diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&table.site, format!("search {} requires a text or enum column on {}", field, table.name), format!("declare `{field}: text <=2000` or `{field}: enum('one', 'two')` before `search {field}`")).guide("search"));
                    }
                }
                let mut indexes = vec![IndexDecl { name: "byId".into(), columns: vec!["id".into()], unique: true, refusal: None, words: None, computed: BTreeMap::new(), site: table.site.clone() }];
                for index in &table.indexes {
                    if indexes.iter().any(|existing| existing.name == index.name) {
                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&index.site, format!("duplicate index {}.{}", table.name, index.name), "name one ordering explicitly"));
                        ok = false;
                        continue;
                    }
                    for column in &index.columns {
                        if index.computed.contains_key(column) { continue; }
                        if column != "id" && !columns.contains_key(column) {
                            diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&index.site, format!("index {}.{} names unknown column {column}", table.name, index.name), name_rewrite(column, columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)));
                            ok = false;
                        }
                    }
                    for field in index.words.iter().flat_map(|(_, fields)| fields) {
                        match columns.get(field) {
                            Some(ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Text { .. } | TypeKind::Enum { .. }, .. }) => {}
                            // The table stays in the catalog: one line is
                            // wrong, and its readers should hear about that
                            // line, not about a table that vanished.
                            Some(other) => diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&index.site, format!("index {}.{} tokenizes {field}, which is {}, not text or enum", table.name, index.name, crate::print::scalar_type(other)), format!("words(<a text or enum column of {}>)", table.name))),
                            None => diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&index.site, format!("index {}.{} tokenizes unknown column {field}", table.name, index.name), name_rewrite(field, columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None))),
                        }
                    }
        {
            diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site, format!("column {}.{} references unknown table {table}", decl.name, column.name), name_rewrite(table, catalog.tables.keys().map(String::as_str), Some("declare the table, or use id for an opaque identity"))));
        }
        columns.insert(column.name.clone(), scalar_to_column(&column.type_));
    }
    let mut recorded = BTreeMap::new();
    for (path, kind) in &info.recorded {
        let (reference, column) = path.split_once('.').expect("admitted path");
        let TypeKind::Ref { table } = &info.columns[reference].kind else { unreachable!("admitted reference") };
        recorded.insert(path.clone(), core::RecordedField { reference: reference.into(), table: table.clone(), column: column.into() });
        columns.insert(path.clone(), scalar_to_column(kind));
    }
    columns.extend(info.captures.iter().map(|(name, ty)| (name.clone(), scalar_to_column(ty))));
    let mut indexes = BTreeMap::new();
    let mut constraints = Vec::new();
    for index in &info.indexes {
        let mut bound = core::Index::of(&index.columns.iter().map(String::as_str).collect::<Vec<_>>(), index.unique);
        for (position, column) in index.columns.iter().enumerate() {
            if let Some(expr) = index.computed.get(column) {
                match super::time_indexes::key(&decl.name, &info.columns, column, expr) {
                    Ok(key) => bound.components[position] = core::IndexComponent::Calendar(key),
                    Err(_) => {} // The catalog has already located this refusal.
                }
            }
        }
        if let Some((position, fields)) = &index.words {
            bound.components.insert(*position, core::IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields: fields.clone(), contract: serde_json::Value::String(snapback4_core::words::CONTRACT.to_owned()) });
        }
        indexes.insert(index.name.clone(), bound);
        if index.unique && index.name != "byId" {
            constraints.push(Constraint::Unique { index: index.name.clone() });
        }
    }
    let mut rules = Rules::deny();
    for effect in [Effect::Read, Effect::Insert, Effect::Update, Effect::Delete] {
        let rule = decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == effect);

    /// A heap-bound allocation can be written; the row validator checks its
    /// actual length. It cannot claim a smaller bound inside an inline helper.
    pub(crate) fn writable_to(&self, expected: &Ty) -> bool {
        match (self, expected) {
            (Ty::Omittable(a),b) => a.as_ref().clone().optional().writable_to(b),
            (a,Ty::Omittable(b)) => a.writable_to(b),
            (Ty::Optional(a), Ty::Optional(b)) => a.writable_to(b),
            (a, Ty::Optional(b)) => a.writable_to(b) || *a == Ty::Null,
            (Ty::Text(u32::MAX), Ty::Text(_)) => true,
            (Ty::List(a, am), Ty::List(b, bm)) => a.writable_to(b) && (*am == u32::MAX || am <= bm),
            _ => self.assignable_to(expected),
        }
    }
