    30	    creator.fields.iter().filter(|f|bound.schema.tables[&creator.table].immutable_fields().any(|c|c==f.name)).find(|f|field_value(f).is_some_and(|e|crate::print::expression(e)==crate::print::expression(value))).map(|f|f.name.clone())
    31	}
    32	fn normalize(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,creator:&Write<'_>,current:&Write<'_>,e:&Expr,creation:bool)->Option<String>{
    33	    let mut e=e.clone();let origins=if current.table==creator.table&&current.op==WriteOp::Update{
    34	        current.keys.first().filter(|_|primary(bound,indexes,&current.table,&current.site,&current.keys)).and_then(|k|if k.column.as_deref()==Some("id"){named_id(bound,indexes,&k.value,current.p,&creator.table)}else{id_value(bound,indexes,&k.value,current.p,&creator.table)}).into_iter().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()
    35	    }else{
    36	        current.fields.iter().filter(|f|matches!(bound.schema.tables[&current.table].columns.get(&f.name).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Ref{table}) if table==&creator.table)).filter_map(field_value).filter_map(|e|id_value(bound,indexes,e,current.p,&creator.table)).collect()
    37	    };
    38	    super::super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut e,&mut |e|{
    39	        let field=if creation{stable_field(bound,creator,e)}else if let Expr::Member{base,name,..}=e{
    40	            read(base).filter(|_|bound.schema.tables[&creator.table].immutable_fields().any(|c|c==name)).filter(|r|r.table==creator.table&&row_id(bound,indexes,r,current.p).is_some_and(|id|origins.contains(&id))).and_then(|_|creator.fields.iter().find(|f|f.name==*name)).and_then(field_value).and_then(|v|stable_field(bound,creator,v))
    41	        }else{None};
    42	        if let Some(field)=field{*e=Expr::Name{name:format!("$created_{field}"),site:e.site().clone()};}
    43	    });
    44	    fn known(e:&Expr)->bool{match e{Expr::Literal{..}=>true,Expr::Name{name,..}=>name.starts_with("$created_"),Expr::Assert{value,..}=>known(value),_=>false}}
    45	    known(&e).then(||crate::print::expression(&e))
    46	}
    47	fn identity(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,creator:&Write<'_>,current:&Write<'_>,r:&ast::ReadExpr,creation:bool)->Option<String>{
    48	    let t=bound.schema.tables.get(&r.table)?;
    49	    let names=indexes.get(&(r.table.clone(),r.site.to_string()))?;if names.len()!=1{return None;}
    50	    let index=t.index(names.first()?)?;if !index.unique||!index.is_plain(){return None;}let columns=index.columns();
    51	    let mut pins=BTreeMap::new();
    52	    for (position,k) in r.keys.iter().enumerate(){let column=k.column.clone().or_else(||columns.get(position).cloned())?;pins.insert(column,normalize(bound,indexes,creator,current,&k.value,creation)?);}
    53	    Some(serde_json::to_string(&columns.iter().map(|c|Some((c,pins.get(c)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?).unwrap())
    54	}
    55	pub(super) fn checks(bound:&Bound,indexes:&Indexes,creator:&Write<'_>,current:&Write<'_>,writes:&[Write<'_>],creation:bool)->BTreeMap<String,BTreeSet<(String,String)>>{
    56	    let mut out=BTreeMap::<String,BTreeSet<(String,String)>>::new();
    57	    if !creation&&writes.iter().any(|w|std::ptr::eq(w.p,current.p)&&w.table==creator.table&&matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert|WriteOp::Delete)){return out;}
    58	
    59	    let mutable=|table:&str,column:&str|bound.schema.tables.get(table).is_some_and(|t|t.columns.contains_key(column)&&!t.immutable_fields().any(|c|c==column))&&writes.iter().any(|o|o.table==table&&(o.op==WriteOp::Delete||matches!(o.op,WriteOp::Update|WriteOp::Upsert)&&o.fields.iter().any(|f|f.name==column)));
    60	    for condition in conditions(&current.context){walk(condition,|e|{
    61	        let (r,columns)=match e{
    62	            Expr::Member{base,name,..}=>{let Some(r)=read(base)else{return;};(r,vec![name.clone()])},
    63	            Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=>{let mut cols=r.keys.iter().filter_map(|k|k.column.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();if writes.iter().any(|o|o.table==r.table&&o.op==WriteOp::Delete){cols.push("existence".into());}(r,cols)},_=>return,
    64	        };
    65	        if r.table==creator.table||!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&r.table).is_empty(){return;}
    66	        let entity=match identity(bound,indexes,creator,current,r,creation){Some(entity)=>entity,None if creation=>format!("$unknown:{}",crate::print::expression(&Expr::Read(r.clone()))),None=>return};
    67	        for column in columns{if column=="existence"||mutable(&r.table,&column){out.entry(r.table.clone()).or_default().insert((column,entity.clone()));}}
    68	    });}out
    69	}

   193	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#169-day-two-precision-and-destructive-evidence-questions — optionality alone is not mandatory evidence.
   194	fn conjuncts(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(conjuncts).collect(),_=>vec![rule]}}
   195	fn required_media(bound:&Bound,table:&str,column:&str)->bool{
   196	    fn nonnull(rule:&RuleExpr,column:&str)->bool{
   197	        let pair=match rule{RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>Some((a,b)),RuleExpr::Not(r)=>if let RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)=r.as_ref(){Some((a,b))}else{None},_=>None};
   198	        pair.is_some_and(|(a,b)|[(a,b),(b,a)].iter().any(|(a,b)|matches!(a,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column)&&matches!(b,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null))))
   199	    }
   200	    fn mandatory(kind:&ColumnType)->bool{match kind{ColumnType::Image{..}|ColumnType::File{..}|ColumnType::Video{..}=>true,ColumnType::ListRange{of,min,..}=>*min>0&&mandatory(of),_=>false}}
   201	    mandatory(&bound.schema.tables[table].columns[column])||mandatory(bound.schema.tables[table].columns[column].required())&&bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==table&&i.target=="always").filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).flat_map(conjuncts).any(|r|nonnull(r,column))
   202	}
   203	fn approves(w:&Write<'_>,bound:&Bound)->bool{
   204	    w.fields.iter().any(|f|match field_value(f){
   205	        Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(v),..})=>matches!(bound.schema.tables[&w.table].columns.get(&f.name).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Enum{..}))&&["approved","accepted","authorized","granted","issued","active","confirmed","paid","signed"].contains(&v.as_str()),
   206	        Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Bool(true),..})=>["approv","authoriz"].iter().any(|part|f.name.to_lowercase().contains(part)),_=>false,
   207	    })
   208	}
   209	fn stale_notes(bound:&Bound,writes:&[Write<'_>],out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>){
   210	    let mut groups=BTreeMap::<(String,String),(Site,BTreeSet<String>,BTreeSet<String>,BTreeSet<String>)>::new();
   211	    let indexes=eligibility::indexes(bound);
   212	    for w in writes.iter().filter(|w|matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert)){
   213	        let dependencies=eligibility::checks(bound,&indexes,w,w,writes,true);
   214	        for (dep,checks) in dependencies{
   215	            let columns=checks.iter().map(|(c,_)|c.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();for later in writes.iter().filter(|o|o.name!=w.name&&approves(o,bound)&&(o.table==w.table&&o.op==WriteOp::Update||matches!(o.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert)&&bound.schema.tables[&o.table].columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),ColumnType::Ref{table} if table==&w.table)))){
   216	            let rechecked=eligibility::checks(bound,&indexes,w,later,writes,false);
   217	            if rechecked.get(&dep).is_some_and(|current|checks.is_subset(current)){continue;}
   218	            let g=groups.entry((w.table.clone(),dep.clone())).or_insert_with(||(later.site.clone(),BTreeSet::new(),BTreeSet::new(),BTreeSet::new()));
   219	            if (&later.site.file,later.site.line,later.site.col)<(&g.0.file,g.0.line,g.0.col){g.0=later.site.clone();}
   220	            g.1.insert(format!("{} at {}",w.name,w.site));g.2.insert(format!("{} at {}",later.name,later.site));g.3.extend(columns.clone());
   221	        }}
   222	    }
   223	    for ((table,dep),(site,creators,decisions,columns)) in groups{note(out,&site,"N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK",format!("{table}: creation by {} checks mutable {dep}.{}, but approval/activation {} does not recheck every column for the same applicant/resource",creators.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "),columns.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(","),decisions.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),"decide whether eligibility must remain current at this decision; recheck it or declare independent intent and test create -> eligibility change -> approval");}
   224	}
   225	fn evidence_notes(bound:&Bound,writes:&[Write<'_>],out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>){
   226	    for (child,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
   227	        let media=t.columns.iter().filter(|(c,k)|k.contains_asset()&&required_media(bound,child,c)).map(|(c,_)|c.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();if media.is_empty(){continue;}
   228	        for (reference,kind) in &t.columns{let ColumnType::Ref{table:parent}=kind.required()else{continue;};
   229	            let declared=required_child(bound,parent,child,reference);
   230	            let created_together=writes.iter().any(|w|w.table==*child&&matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert)&&writes.iter().any(|p|std::ptr::eq(p.p,w.p)&&p.table==*parent&&matches!(p.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert)&&w.fields.iter().find(|f|&f.name==reference).and_then(field_value).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="id"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Write{table,site,..} if table==parent&&site==&p.site))))&&media.iter().any(|c|w.fields.iter().find(|f|&f.name==c).and_then(field_value).is_some_and(|e|w.p.args.iter().any(|a|!a.type_.optional&&uses(e,&a.name)))));
   231	            if !declared&&!created_together{continue;}
   232	            let removing=writes.iter().filter(|w|w.table==*child&&(w.op==WriteOp::Delete||matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Update|WriteOp::Upsert)&&w.fields.iter().any(|f|{
   233	                if &f.name==reference{return !field_value(f).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name==reference&&same_target(base,w)));}
   234	                media.contains(&f.name)&&match field_value(f){Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Null,..})=>true,Some(Expr::Array{items,..})=>items.is_empty(),_=>false}
   235	            }))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   236	            if removing.is_empty(){continue;}
   237	            let operations=removing.iter().map(|w|format!("{} ({})",w.name,if w.op==WriteOp::Delete{"delete"}else{"detach/clear"})).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
   238	            note(out,&removing[0].site,"N_EVIDENCE_REMOVAL",format!("{child}.{reference} -> {parent}: {operations} can remove or move evidence {}; could this remove a parent's last required photo/file? Required child fields alone do not require a surviving child.",media.join(",")),"check the specification for last-child retention; guard deletion/detachment transactionally, replace evidence or retire the parent, then declare independent parent integrity intent and run test");
   239	        }
   240	    }
   241	}

   243	fn required_child(bound:&Bound,parent:&str,child:&str,reference:&str)->bool{
   244	    bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==parent&&i.target=="always").filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).flat_map(conjuncts).any(|r|if let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=r{
   245	        table==child&&(bound.schema.tables[table].index(index).is_some_and(|idx|idx.components.iter().zip(key).any(|(c,v)|c.column().map(String::as_str)==Some(reference)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c=="id")))||equalities.iter().any(|(c,v)|c==reference&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c=="id")))
   246	    }else{false})
   247	}

   249	// The obligation names a relationship and any literal discriminator, not every
   250	// attachment in the child table (e.g. required checkout vs optional return).
   251	fn mandatory_parent_for(bound:&Bound,w:&Write<'_>,writes:&[Write<'_>])->bool{
   252	    fn linked(w:&Write<'_>,reference:&str,parent:&Write<'_>)->bool{w.fields.iter().find(|f|f.name==reference).and_then(field_value).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="id"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Write{table,site,..} if table==&parent.table&&site==&parent.site)))}
   253	    fn literal(e:&Expr,v:&snapback4_core::Value)->bool{match(e,v){
   254	        (Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(a),..},snapback4_core::Value::String(b))=>a==b,
   255	        (Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Bool(a),..},snapback4_core::Value::Bool(b))=>a==b,
   256	        (Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Null,..},snapback4_core::Value::Null)=>true,
   257	        (Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Number(a),..},snapback4_core::Value::Int(b))=>a.parse::<i64>().ok()==Some(*b),_=>false,
   258	    }}
   259	    for (reference,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[&w.table].columns{let ColumnType::Ref{table:parent}=kind.required()else{continue;};
   260	        for p in writes.iter().filter(|p|std::ptr::eq(p.p,w.p)&&p.table==*parent&&matches!(p.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert)&&linked(w,reference,p)){
   261	            for policy in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==*parent&&i.target=="always").filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).flat_map(conjuncts){
   262	                let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=policy else{continue;};if table!=&w.table{continue;}
   263	                let pairs=super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
   264	                if !pairs.iter().any(|(c,v)|c==reference&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c=="id")){continue;}
   265	                let matches=|candidate:&Write<'_>|linked(candidate,reference,p)&&pairs.iter().all(|(column,term)|{
   266	                    if column==reference{return matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c=="id");}
   267	                    let RuleTerm::Literal(value)=term else{return false;};candidate.fields.iter().find(|f|&f.name==column).and_then(field_value).is_some_and(|e|literal(e,value))
   268	                });
   269	                if !matches(w){continue;}
   270	                let already=writes.iter().any(|other|std::ptr::eq(other.p,w.p)&&other.table==w.table&&other.site!=w.site&&other.op==WriteOp::Insert&&!other.conditional&&matches(other)&&ordinary_unconditional(other.p,&other.site));
   271	                if !already{return true;}
   272	            }
   273	        }
   274	    }false
   275	}

   277	fn ordinary_unconditional(p:&ast::ProgramDecl,site:&Site)->bool{
   278	    for statement in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){
   279	        match statement{
   280	            Stmt::Expr{value,..}|Stmt::Bind{value,..}|Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{
   281	                if matches!(value,Expr::Write{op:WriteOp::Insert,site:s,if_absent:false,condition:None,..} if s==site){return true;}
   282	                if matches!(statement,Stmt::Return{..}){return false;}
   283	            },
   284	            Stmt::If{..}|Stmt::For{..}|Stmt::As{..}|Stmt::Break{..}|Stmt::Continue{..}=>return false,_=>{},
   285	        }
   286	    }false
   287	}

   291	}

   293	fn links_created_parent(w:&Write<'_>,writes:&[&Write<'_>])->bool{
   294	    writes.iter().any(|p|w.fields.iter().any(|f|matches!(field_value(f),Some(Expr::Member{base,name,..}) if name=="id"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Write{table,site,..} if table==&p.table&&site==&p.site))))
   295	}
    19	    fn necessary(rule:&RuleExpr)->RuleExpr{match rule{
    20	        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(necessary).collect()),
    21	        _ if super::operation::safe(rule)=>rule.clone(),_=>RuleExpr::Allow,
    22	    }}
    23	    // Recover only column-specific conditional guards; ordinary table-wide
    24	    // differences keep their existing inventory code and priority.
    25	    let mut specific=BTreeMap::<(String,String,String,String),(bool,Vec<RuleExpr>)>::new();
    26	    for doc in &documents{for p in &doc.programs{if p.kind!=ast::ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
    27	        let plain=super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p);
    28	        let columns=plain.iter().filter_map(|(e,_)|if let ast::Expr::Write{op:ast::WriteOp::Update,fields,..}=e{Some(fields.iter().map(|f|f.name.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>())}else{None}).flatten().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    29	        for column in columns{for ((write,context),(_,base)) in super::authority_walk::column_writes(&catalog,p,&column).into_iter().zip(&plain){
    30	            let ast::Expr::Write{op:ast::WriteOp::Update,table,fields,..}=write else{continue;};
    31	            if !fields.iter().any(|f|f.name==column){continue;}
    32	            if plain.iter().filter(|(e,_)|matches!(e,ast::Expr::Write{table:t,..} if t==&table)).count()!=1{continue;}
    33	            let requirements=super::operation::requirements(&catalog,&catalog.tables[&table],&context);
    34	            let restriction=RuleExpr::And(requirements.into_iter().chain([bound.schema.tables[&table].update_rule_judged().into_owned()]).collect());
    35	            let entry=specific.entry((p.site.file.clone(),p.name.clone(),table,column.clone())).or_default();entry.0|=context.body.len()>base.body.len();entry.1.push(necessary(&restriction));
    36	        }}
    37	    }}
    38	    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut replaced=BTreeSet::new();
    39	    for (creator,name,op,table,fields,site,admission) in &writes{
    40	        if !matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert){continue;}
    41	        for ((file,editor,target,column),(conditional,guards)) in &specific{
    42	            if !conditional{continue;}
    43	            let guard=RuleExpr::Or(guards.clone());
    44	            if target!=table||!fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column&&supplied(f,creator)){continue;}
    45	            let guard=necessary(&guard);
    46	            let module=std::path::Path::new(file).file_stem().and_then(|s|s.to_str()).unwrap_or("");
    47	            let operation=super::super::operation_name(module,editor);
    48	            let Some(program)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==operation)else{continue;};
    49	            let dependencies=kernel::dependencies(&guard);
    50	            if snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&program.program).iter().any(|(table,_)|dependencies.contains(table)){continue;}
    51	            if kernel::implies(admission,&guard)!=Ok(false)||!seen.insert((creator.site.file.clone(),name.clone(),table.clone(),BTreeSet::from([column.clone()]),guard.quarry())){continue;}
    52	            let audience=bound.schema.tables[table].column_reads.get(column);
    53	            let private=audience.is_some_and(|r|kernel::implies(admission,r)==Ok(false));
    54	            let code=if private{"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"}else{"N_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"};
    55	            let detail=if private{format!("; write-only private payload: creator admission does not imply column read audience {}",audience.unwrap().quarry())}else{String::new()};
    56	            out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,format!("{name}: argument-supplied {table}.{column} is open at create/upsert, but {operation} requires {} when this column changes{detail}",guard.quarry()),"confirm this column's creation authority against the specification; apply its change guard at creation too when required, declare independent column write intent, then run test with a non-null value").guide("security"));
    57	            replaced.insert((creator.site.file.clone(),name.clone(),table.clone(),column.clone()));
    58	        }
    59	    }
    60	    seen.clear();
    61	    for (creator,name,op,table,fields,site,admission) in &writes{

  2315	**Optional uploads.** Optional arguments and optional columns are a valid model,
  2316	including deliberate v2 loosening and exceptions such as a lockout without proof.
  2317	Do not gate them merely because their names suggest evidence. Gate an optional
  2318	image/file argument flowing to a creating media column only when that column is
  2319	required, or a direct positive conjunct of an authored `table always` invariant
  2320	requires that exact column to be non-null. Flatten conjunctions and recognize
  2321	`field != null` and the exactly equivalent `not (field = null)` in either operand
  2322	order. Other negation, an OR, an unrelated column, comment, product document or unknown predicate is not
  2323	a mandatory-media declaration. No natural-language specification parsing, deploy
  2324	diff exemption, lane exemption or new intent language is introduced. Required
  2325	child media with optional arguments does not, by itself, require a child row.
  2326	A child-local non-null branch is valid optional attachment creation, including
  2327	040's return photo, and cannot fail check on that basis. Gate missing parent
  2328	evidence only when an independent parent `always exists child[...]` conjunct
  2329	pins the exact parent-reference relationship and the operation creates that parent.
  2330	Such a child-only guard cannot satisfy the explicit parent-level obligation.
  2331	Match the exact created parent reference and every additional literal child-probe
  2332	filter (e.g. checkout vs return). Unknown filters do not establish this gate.
  2333	An unconditional ordinary sibling insertion satisfying that same probe discharges
  2334	the obligation; an unrelated optional attachment must not be gated for it.
  2335	Conditional or if-absent sibling insertions cannot establish that discharge.
  2336	Without that obligation, parent-plus-conditional-child creation may ask the existing
  2337	advisory optional-evidence question, but never imposes an acknowledgment.
  2338	 Existing dominating non-null
  2339	guards across every creating context still suppress the question. Required scalar media (or a required nonempty list of required media)
  2340	with required arguments needs no optional-upload review. A required list allowing
  2341	zero elements is not mandatory evidence. Existing generic optional

**NOT READY.** Static review found three material counterexamples:

1. **Unconditional helper-validated child creation still gates.** In [creation_advice.rs:72](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b22-sec7/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/creation_advice.rs:72), `parent_required` bypasses the direct-value restriction. Starting from the `CHILD` test fixture, replace its conditional insertion with:
   ```quarry
   insert photos {parent:p.id,photo:checked(photo)}
   ```
   where `checked` returns `photo ! else REQUIRED`, and retain the parent integrity invariant. The detector emits `N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE`: it recognizes neither a direct assertion nor a non-null requirement. Yet the child is unconditional, and null aborts the transaction. There is no skipped-child path.

2. **Multiple parent references collapse eligibility identity.** [eligibility.rs:36](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b22-sec7/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/creation_advice/eligibility.rs:36) collects every reference to the created table into one `origins` set. Consider an approval child containing `application:target.id` and `checkedApplication:other.id`. A requirement checking only `operatorFlags[other.owner]` normalizes to the creator’s eligibility identity and suppresses the question for this approval path, although `target.owner` remains unchecked. Ambiguous relationships must retain the question or be checked separately.

3. **Upload dedup still omits source site.** [creation_advice.rs:72](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b22-sec7/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/creation_advice.rs:72) keys `seen` by qualified operation and argument only. Two inserts in one mutation using the same optional photo argument, targeting two tables with independent non-null intent, produce only the first gate.
